## 12. Found while closing out #3665 (October 2026)

Findings 8 and 9 were fixed in the two maintained risk calculators. A final pass over everything the
chart's Calculators index opens found the same class of fault in a third calculator, and the two
filed defects still live in public copies of the fixed pages.

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 129 | **The Framingham/UKPDS risk calculator answers with its highest-risk band for a box it cannot use.** `riskcalc/js/js.js` read every box with `parseFloat`: a blank or mistyped systolic or HDL became `NaN`, every `<=` in the band ladders compared false, and the page highlighted the >=160 row and the highest cholesterol-ratio column; an HDL of 0 divided by zero to the same column, `1,2` was read as 1, and an age outside 30-75 was clamped and the box silently rewritten. The UKPDS page threw on a `NaN` systolic (and on any decimal between 149 and 150) and left the previous calculation's table and advice on screen. The calculator also ignored the `sex`/`age` the index passes, so it opened on a 55-year-old man for every patient | Source reading, then the page's own script under a fake DOM. Fixed: every box goes through `readRiskInputs()` (the age through the shared `clinicalCalculatorAge.js`), which refuses blank, non-numeric and out-of-range values with a message naming the box and its range, clears every figure and highlight, and computes nothing; the UKPDS ladder covers every value below 150 in the 140 row; the chart's sex and age prefill the form and carry to the diabetic page. `scripts/framingham-ukpds-calculator.test.js` pins the guard; `clinical-calculators-playwright-checks.js` opens the calculator from the chart and asserts the prefill, valid answers and every refusal | `fixed` |
| 130 | **Public static copies of the fixed calculators still carried findings 8 and 9.** `encounter/calculators/OsteoporoticFracture.htm` and `CoronaryArteryDiseaseRiskPrediction.html` (and `SimpleCalculator.htm`) were duplicates of the maintained `WEB-INF` pages that nothing links to, still served to a bookmark, and still ran the unguarded `age <= 54` ladder | Source search: no caller in `src/main`; `grep "age <= 54"` matched only these copies. Fixed: each is now a redirect to its maintained `encounter/calculators/View*` route that forwards the legacy `?sex=&age=` query and fragment, as `GeneralCalculators.htm` already was. `framingham-ukpds-calculator.test.js` runs each redirect; `legacy-calculator-bookmarks-playwright-checks.js` follows them on a deployment and proves the maintained page refuses a blank age | `fixed` |

## 13. Found while resolving #3446: the OAuth web-service surfaces (October 2026)

Rows 131 to 134 were reproduced on `carlos-emr 2026.09.0~snapshot26` built from `release/2026.08`
and installed into an Ubuntu 26.04 container behind the packaged nginx + ModSecurity front door.
Each was confirmed fixed both on that install upgraded in place and on a fresh install
of the #3446 build (see
[deb-install-validation.md](deb-install-validation.md#oauth-rest-surfaces-validation-2026-10-08)).
Rows 132 to 134 were hidden behind row 131: nothing could reach them while the servers were unpublished.
`oauth-rest-surfaces-playwright-checks.js` fails on rows 131, 133 and 134.
Row 132's guard is its unit test: once row 133's fix tells CXF the consent page is the response,
CXF no longer probes the output stream on that page. Row 135 is a static finding and stays open.

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 131 | `/ws/oauth/*` (the OAuth 1.0a handshake) and `/ws/services/*` (the OAuth data API) answer CXF's "No service was found." 404 on every packaged and devcontainer install. `OscarSpringContextLoader` refreshes the root context itself from `applicationContext.xml` plus the `ModuleNames` files, so `web.xml`'s `classpath:applicationContext*.xml` is never read, and no shipped configuration lists `REST` (the pre-turnkey `release/config` script wrote `ModuleNames=REST`). Listing it was no remedy: `applicationContextREST.xml` reused `spring_ws.xml`'s `jaxb`, `jacksonObjectMapper` and `jsonProvider` ids, so loading it replaced the session `/ws/rs` mapper | Unfixed install: `curl -sk -X POST https://localhost/carlos/ws/oauth/initiate` and `GET /ws/services/oauth/info` return 404 while `/ws/rs/status/checkIfAuthed` returns 401. With `ModuleNames=REST` added, both servers publish (400 / 401), but `/ws/rs/demographics/1` changes `patientStatusDate` from `1690243200000` to `"2023-07-25"`. Fixed: REST is a core context (`OscarSpringContextLoaderUnitTest`) and the OAuth providers have their own ids (`OAuthRestContextIsolationUnitTest`, which fails on the old XML with the same `"2023-07-25"`) | `fixed` |
| 132 | The OAuth consent page `GET /ws/oauth/authorize?oauth_token=...` answers HTTP 500 `IllegalStateException: getWriter() has already been called`. CXF closes the void JAX-RS response with `getOutputStream().close()` and swallows the refusal. `PrivacyStatementAppendingFilter`'s wrapper had already recorded the stream as obtained, so the filter then appended its statement through it | Packaged-install journal: the trace through `PrivacyStatementAppendingFilter.printConfidentialityStatement`. CXF 4.1.8 bytecode of `AbstractHTTPDestination.closeResponseOutputStream` (catches `IllegalStateException`). `PrivacyStatementAppendingFilterUnitTest.shouldAppendThroughWriter_whenCallerProbesStreamAfterWriter` fails before the fix with the same exception | `fixed` |
| 133 | With row 132 fixed, the consent page answers 204 with an empty body, so a provider cannot approve an application. The forwarded JSP's output is held uncommitted by the response-wrapping filters, and CXF finishes the void method with 204 and Content-Length 0 | Raw `GET /ws/oauth/authorize` with a valid request token: `204`, `bodylen=0`, through `:443` and directly against Tomcat. Fixed: after the forward, `AuthorizeResource` sets `AbstractHTTPDestination.REQUEST_REDIRECTED`, which `flushHeaders` honours (CXF 4.1.8 bytecode). Afterwards the page answers `200` with its 9.4 KB body. `AuthorizeResourceUnitTest.shouldMarkResponseWritten_whenConsentPageForwarded` | `fixed` |
| 134 | A correctly signed `/ws/services` data call, for example `GET /ws/services/demographics/{id}`, answers 500 "Authentication info is not available", and every OAuth call creates a session. `webserv.rest.AbstractServiceImpl.getLoggedInInfo()` read the session first and fell back to the request principal that `OAuthInterceptor` sets only when the session held a provider-less principal | Packaged-install journal: the trace at `AbstractServiceImpl.getLoggedInInfo` from `DemographicService.requireDemographicPrivilege`, after `/ws/services/oauth/info` (a different base class) had answered 200 for the same token. `AbstractServiceImplLoggedInInfoEndpointTest` drives a CXF local-transport chain and fails before the fix for the OAuth-only and request-plus-session shapes | `fixed` |
| 135 | Administration ▸ REST Clients never shows a client's secret. The add response and the list omit it (`ClientManage2Action.ServiceClientListEntry`), so an operator cannot give an integrator the secret its signatures need without reading `ServiceClient.clientSecret` from the database | `ClientManage2Action.java` (`ServiceClientListEntry` has id, name, key, URI and lifetime; the add branch returns only method, name, success and error); `admin/api/clients.jsp` renders key and URI only. `oauth-rest-surfaces-playwright-checks.js` therefore seeds its client with SQL. Not changed by #3446; it needs a decision on how a secret is disclosed (for example, once, at creation) | `open` |

## 14. Found by the 2026.08.0-alpha19 promotion validation (October 2026)

Reproduced on `carlos-emr 2026.08.0~alpha19` built from a local merge of `release/2026.08`
(9784de2710) into `main` (bef663c9a4) and installed into Ubuntu 26.04 systemd containers
with the Ontario demo dataset (136-144) and the British Columbia one (145-147), through the
packaged front door (`:443`). See
[deb-install-validation.md](deb-install-validation.md#promotion-validation-2026080-alpha19-2026-10-08).
Rows 137 to 139, 141 and 144 are cited by `scripts/lib/server-log-baseline.tsv` (142's entry was retired with its fix), so the
server-log audit reports them as known rather than failing on them.

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 136 | **The month view's week numbers are links to an error page.** `appointmentprovideradminmonth.jsp` renders each week number as a link to `providercontrol?...&displaymode=week&dboperation=searchapptweek`. `week` has never been in `providercontrol.jsp`'s dispatch table: before #4381 the link included nothing and showed a blank page; since #4381 closed unknown modes with `sendError(404)`, a click lands on "CARLOS has encountered an unexpected error ... CARLOS Error: 404". Either the links go or a week view is restored | Live: Schedule ▸ Month showed 10 week-number links; clicking the first answered HTTP 404 with the error page. Source: `appointmentprovideradminmonth.jsp:830` and `:934`; dispatch table `providercontrol.jsp:165-180`. Pinned by `schedule-month-week-links`. Filed as #4540. **Fixed (#4540):** A week number on a month that shows one provider opens that provider's week view, the day sheet with `weekView=true` that the "W" button of a provider column opens (built by `ScheduleWeekLink`, the month JSP now closes its anchor too). The link asks the sheet for that provider alone (`view=1&curProvider=`), because the sheet otherwise lists the viewer's saved schedule view and would render another provider's week. A month that shows All providers or a group has no single provider to open, and a provider the viewer's saved schedule view would hide is not linked either, so those week numbers are plain text. | fixed |
| 137 | **Six `providercontrol` display modes answer HTTP 500.** `encounter`, `saveencounter`, `savebill`, `savedeletetemplate`, `ar1` and `ar2` are still in the dispatch table but name JSPs that no longer exist (`providerencounter.jsp`, `providersaveencounter.jsp`, `providersavebill.jsp`, a relative `providertemplate.jsp`, `formar1_99_12.jsp`, `formar2_99_08.jsp`), so the include throws `ServletException: JSP file [...] not found`. Nothing links to them; #4381 made unknown modes 404 but left these entries | Live: `GET /provider/providercontrol?displaymode=<mode>` answered 500 for each, and the journal logged one `ErrorPageLogger` ERROR per request naming the missing JSP. Filed as #4541. | issue-filed |
| 138 | **Printing an appointment receipt logs an ERROR that is not one.** `PrintAppointmentReceipt2Action.java:173` calls `logger.error("user home: " + ...)` on every print. It is debug output at the wrong level, and it puts a false ERROR in front of every operator reading the journal | Live: two `ERROR pageUtil.PrintAppointmentReceipt2Action` lines during `appointment-lifecycle`'s Update & Receipt step, which passed. Listed in #3048 (child #2649, closed as a duplicate of it). | issue-filed |
| 139 | **Deleting a pharmacy that has link history logs "UNKNOWN PHARMACY TO UNLINK" at ERROR.** `RxPharmacyData.deletePharmacy` walks every `DemographicPharmacy` row for the pharmacy, inactive history included, and calls `unlinkPharmacy` for each; `unlinkPharmacy` only finds ACTIVE rows, so the second row of a patient's link/unlink/relink history logs the error. The delete itself is correct | Live: one ERROR during `pharmacy-editor-workflow`, whose database assertions all passed. Source: `RxPharmacyData.java:117-124`, `DemographicPharmacyDaoImpl.java:117-151`. Filed as #4542. Live again on 2026-10-18: the current-boot server audit of the packaged target records the known DemographicPharmacyDaoImpl:149 ERROR while the pharmacy-editor workflow passes its unlink/deactivate assertions. Tracking audit 2026-10-18: [#4542](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/4542); existing open ticket, no matching active fix found in the targeted search. | issue-filed |
| 140 | **An eForm emailed from one patient's window can carry another patient's eForm.** The prepared attachments live in ONE session-wide attribute, `emailAttachmentList`: `EmailCompose2Action.java:303` (and `ManageEmails2Action.java:276` on resend) overwrite it, and `EmailSend2Action.java:241` sends whatever it holds at send time. With patient X's composer open, opening an eForm email for patient Y in another window replaces the list, so sending from X's window emails X a PDF of Y's eForm, and the `emailAttachment` row records Y's form on X's email. #4378 isolated the two windows' drafts (sender, subject, body) but not the attachments. A PHI disclosure to the wrong patient; the attachments should be keyed by the compose transaction, as the drafts now are | Live: `eform-email-two-windows-playwright-checks.js` steps 1-2 pass (two composers, each listing its own patient's eForm); step 3 fails with "X's email carried an eForm that is not X's" — the SMTP sink received X's address and the `emailAttachment` row's `eform_data.demographic_no` was Y's Filed as #4425. Fixed on release/2026.08 by #4444 (each email's attachments are bound to its own window and patient). Re-verified live on the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package (release/2026.08 at 0c43d2f862): `eform-email-two-windows` passes all three steps. | `fixed` |
| 141 | **Opening a note's history writes the note's text into the application log, and its audit row loses the patient.** `CaseManagementEntry2Action.history()` (`:2936`) calls the six-argument `LogAction.addLog(provider, action, content, contentId, ip, demographicNo)` with `note.getAuditString()` as the last argument. `LogAction.java:235` parses it as the demographic number, fails, and logs `NumberFormatException: For input string: "<note text and issues>"` at ERROR; the `log` row is written with a NULL demographic id and NULL data. PHI in the journal on every history view, and the access audit can no longer be filtered by patient. `notehistory` (#4307) is a separate path and is not affected | Live: 7 `ERROR ... LogAction` lines with `For input string:` in the packaged journal during `authz-read-patient-lock` and `authz-read-chart-note-history`; the call site is the only one that passes data in the `demographicNo` slot Filed as #4426. Open PR #4451 fixes it (closes #4426); #4172 lists it too (L335). | `issue-filed` |
| 142 | **The schedule's tab-alert refresh answers 500 for a role without `_msg`.** `tabAlertsRefresh.jsp` renders `MsgNewMessageTag`, which calls `MessagingManagerImpl.getMyInboxMessageCount` (`:217`); that throws `SecurityException` instead of the tag rendering no count, so every refresh logs two ERRORs and the tab badge request fails | Live: 7 `ViewTabAlertsRefresh` 500s in the journal from the `authz-read-*` logins that hold no `_msg` right. Authorization refusal handling for a signed-in user: tracked in umbrella [#4223](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/4223), section B. Fixed by #4696 (#2689): the schedule polls only the tabs it rendered, and `ViewTabAlertsRefresh2Action` accepts only the five tab ids (400 otherwise) and refuses a tab without its menu privilege (403). Live on `release/2026.08` plus #4696, packaged as `2026.09.0~snapshot26+pr2689` and upgraded in place over the unmodified package (Ubuntu 26.04, Ontario demo): `schedule-role-access` passes, and the journal holds no `ViewTabAlertsRefresh` error across the `authz-read-*` and `schedule-*` runs. The unmodified package logged the `_msg` 500 again within minutes of a downgrade. The `scripts/lib/server-log-baseline.tsv` entry is retired. | fixed |
| 143 | **The packaged Ontario demo dataset links ticklers and documents that are not in it to old notes, and keeps routing and attachment rows for documents and eForms that are not in it.** The additive demo artifact carries `casemgmt_note_link` rows with `table_name=10` (tickler) whose tickler ids were filtered out, while the fresh `tickler` table starts its AUTO_INCREMENT low. The first new ticklers take those ids and open an unrelated demo note from their note dialog. The `table_name=5` (document) rows have the same defect: the dataset holds 21 documents, and 37 document ids have a link row but no document row, so the first new documents take those ids. Two more tables have the same defect. `patientLabRouting` holds `DOC` rows for document numbers 55 (6 rows), 79 (1), 80 (1) and 81 (2) and `providerLabRouting` holds `DOC` rows for 55 (2 rows, providers 999998 and 99998), none with a `document` row, so a new document that takes one of those numbers is routed to demographic 1 or 3 and to those providers (`detached-delete-forward-favourites` refuses such a number and fails once the counter reaches 55, and `inbox-file-forward` skips it). `EFormDocs` holds 4,676 attachment rows (documents, labs, forms, HRM reports and eForms) for eForm instance ids 247 to 1063, and the dataset loads no `eform_data` rows, so the first eForm instances saved with an id in that range inherit those attachments; `AddEForm2Action` answers 403 and stores nothing when the saving login may not read one of the inherited attachment types: the save and its attachment sync share one transaction (`:340-351`), `DocumentAttach.syncEFormDocuments` (`:179-189`) passes the stale rows to `AttachmentSelectionAccess.validate`, which throws `SecurityException`, and the action maps that to HTTP 403 (`:358-364`). Demo-only: a production database never loads the artifact. `build-demo-additive.sh` should drop links, routing rows and attachment rows whose target is filtered, or carry the AUTO_INCREMENT of every table whose ids they reference | Live: on a fresh `carlos-ctl demo-data` install, `tickler-note-dialog` showed note 555 on a tickler it had just created; tickler 5 still links note 73 with no tickler 5 in the dataset. Static: `scripts/demo-dataset-contract.test.js` builds the artifact with `build-demo-additive.sh` and finds 3 tickler ids (16 of 17 tickler link rows; the dataset holds one tickler) and 37 document ids (37 of 57 document link rows; 21 documents) with no target row, identically in the Ontario and BC artifacts and in `development.sql` itself. Its four `finding 143` todos pin the tickler and document link halves only, one pair per province (`shouldLinkNoNoteToMissingTickler_in{Ontario,Bc}AdditiveArtifact` and `shouldLinkNoNoteToMissingDocument_in{Ontario,Bc}AdditiveArtifact`), each backed by an undecorated test that the artifact yields link and target rows, so a todo cannot read as fixed on an empty artifact. Nothing pins the routing and attachment halves: no static test reads `patientLabRouting`, `providerLabRouting` or `EFormDocs` from the demo artifact (the `scripts/*.test.js` files that mention those tables use them only as fixtures for other checks' SQL), and no manifest entry carries an `expectedFailure` for this finding. Three checks only work around them: `detached-delete-forward-favourites` refuses a colliding document number, `inbox-file-forward` takes the next number (up to six tries), and `double-submit-eform` moves the eForm id counter past 1063 first, so its control step is not refused 403. Routing rows, read 2026-10-09 from the additive artifact (`/usr/share/carlos-emr/demo/demo-additive-on.sql.gz`): `INSERT IGNORE INTO patientLabRouting` carries `DOC` rows for lab numbers 3, 55, 79, 80 and 81, and `providerLabRouting` for 2, 3 and 55, while `document` holds numbers 1 to 21; the packaged install shows the same rows for 55 and 79 to 81. Attachment rows, same artifact: `INSERT IGNORE INTO EFormDocs` carries 4,676 rows with `fdid` 247 to 1063 and no `eform_data` insert exists. Live, `double-submit-eform` (2026-10-08, packaged install through `:443`, `eform_data` counter below 247): the control step's POST by a login that cannot read the attachment object types answered 403 'The eForm was not saved because you do not have permission to use one or more selected attachments' and stored nothing, in every run after the counter reached the range (5 runs), where 409 was expected; the check now moves the counter past 1063 with an owned row, so the counter on that install cannot be put back. Source: `AddEForm2Action.java:340-364`, `DocumentAttach.java:162-189`. Filed as #4499. **Alpha20 fix:** #4905: repaired development.sql and therefore both additive artifacts: removed 53 orphan note links, 10 patient DOC routes, two provider DOC routes and all 4,676 attachments without eform_data parents. Required tests cover each relation in source/ON/BC artifacts and preserve valid routes. Existing already-loaded databases are not automatically rewritten.  **Residual installed-demo issue:** already-loaded demo databases retain their old orphan rows until explicitly rebuilt or reconciled. This finding remains open for that release disposition; the shipped-source repair does not silently delete operator data. | issue-filed |
| 144 | **Two more debug lines are logged at ERROR, one with prescription text.** `RxWriteScriptForm.setSpecial` (`:266-267`) logs `drug special is either null or empty : <instructions>` with a stack trace whenever the instructions are shorter than six characters ("1 od" is a valid one), so medication instructions reach the journal; the value is still accepted. `labValuesGraph.jsp:240` logs `ARR <n>` on every graph. Like 138, false ERRORs that bury real ones | Live: 8 `RxWriteScriptForm.setSpecial` ERRORs during the Rx stash, favourite and `gap-encounter-rx-add-favourite` checks, all of which passed; one `labValuesGraph_jsp` ERROR during `lab-manual-entry-cumulative`. Filed as #4543. Live again on 2026-10-18: the current-boot server audit records eight RxWriteScriptForm.setSpecial IllegalArgumentException ERROR events during the Rx validation campaign; the existing baseline still identifies this logging defect. Tracking audit 2026-10-18: [#4543](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/4543); existing open ticket, no matching active fix found in the targeted search. | issue-filed |
| 145 | **Every save from the BC adjust bill page adds a backslash before each apostrophe in the billing note.** `BillingReProcessBill2Action` (`:478-481`) re-adds the note on every save of a bill that has one, through `BillingNote.addNote`, which stores `UtilMisc.mysqlEscape(note)` with a parameterized JPA persist: `'` is stored as `\'`, the page shows the stored text, and the next save escapes it again (`patient\'s`, then `patient\\'s`). #4373 made the page reachable again, so this now happens on every Settle, Reprocess or Resubmit; the same helper writes the note from `BillingSaveBilling2Action`, `BillingUpdateBilling2Action` and quick billing. The escape belongs to the old string-built SQL and should go | Live on the BC install: `billing-bc-reprocess-bill`'s note step, run past finding 147, read back `" adjust note: patient\'s card re-read"` after one Settle Bill of `" adjust note: patient's card re-read"` Filed as #4427. | `issue-filed` |
| 146 | **On a BC install the master record's E-Chart, Invoice List and other navigation links are covered by the content pane at their centre.** The BC-only "Check Eligibility" menu (`edit.jsp:1117-1121`, `#menu2`, `style="width:350px"`) renders inline under its link, showing a stray "Loading ..." in the navigation and widening the navigation cell's links to 350px under the 155px column, so `.leftSection` covers each link from x=176. A click on a link's words still works; a click right of them hits the content, and every Playwright check that clicks a navigation link by its centre times out. The link also calls the Teleplan eligibility service on mouseover | Live at 1280x720 on the BC install: `elementFromPoint` at the E-Chart link's centre is `DIV.leftSection`; `browser-surface`, `document-upload` and `echart` (smoke), and `billing-bc-reprocess-bill` before it clicked the link's words, time out on "subtree intercepts pointer events". At 1600x1000 the centre is clear. Introduced with #3691, not by the 2026.08 delta. Filed as #4544. **Fixed in PR #4641 (#4544):** eligibility is outside the navigation layout and opens only on explicit activation in an accessible dialog. The full BC application check passes centred Invoice List and E-Chart clicks at 1280×720 and 1600×1000, with one pending eligibility request and close/reopen behavior (2026-10-14). The disposable BC database validated all 39 canonical common/BC migrations; only the external eligibility response is intercepted, so this is not a Teleplan integration claim. | `fixed` |
| 147 | **The BC adjust bill page throws a TypeError on load for every bill that is not settled.** `onLoad="setfocus();checkDebitRequest();correspondenceNote();"` (`adjustBill.jsp:430`); `checkDebitRequest()` hides `#submitButton`, which is rendered only for a settled bill (`:1214-1217`), so `HideElementById` reads `.style` of null, the handler stops, and `correspondenceNote()` never sets the Correspondence Note box for the bill's correspondence code. Changing the submission code to "E" throws the same way after showing the debit request. The markup predates the delta; #4373 made the page reachable | Live on the BC install: `uncaught TypeError: Cannot read properties of null (reading 'style') at HideElementById ... at checkDebitRequest` on opening an open bill from Bill Status Filed as #4427. Fixed with #4551 (#4427): `HideElementById` and `ShowElementById` skip an element the page did not render, so the onLoad handlers run to the end. | `fixed` |

### Found by the commit-by-commit review of the 2026.08 delta

The 80 production commits in `main..release/2026.08` were read in seven domain groups against the
code as released. Each row was then checked in the source by tracing a realistic request to the
failure; "Live" marks one also reproduced on the packaged British Columbia install. "Made
reachable" means the defective code predates the commit, which made it run again.

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 148 | **The note-revision popup renders the patient's stored name as HTML.** `casemgmt/showHistory.jsp:74-75` prints `${title}` and `${demoName}` as raw EL. #4307 now sends `demographicNo` with every note's "rev" link, so `notehistory` fills `demoName` from `getDemoName`, which is unencoded. A name containing markup becomes live markup in another clinician's session. Made reachable by #4307 | Live: an owned test patient whose first name held an HTML element with an event handler; the `notehistory` popup created the element and the handler ran. `xss-poison-note-history-playwright-checks.js` fails on it with the suite's inert markup (MARKUP-INJECTED, patient first and last name). Fix: encode both values with the CARLOS wrappers Filed as #4417. | `issue-filed` |
| 149 | **Lab Row Display tooltips render lab text as HTML.** `lab/DisplayLabValue.jsp:70,95` attribute-encodes the test name, result, units and range into a boxover `title`; the browser decodes the attribute and `share/javascript/boxover.js:149-150` writes the header and body with `innerHTML`. A lab value or test name carrying markup runs when another provider hovers the row. #4304 restored these tooltips | Source. Fix: HTML-encode before attribute-encoding, or make boxover use `textContent` Filed as #4418. Open PR #4448 HTML-encodes the tooltip payloads. | `issue-filed` |
| 150 | **OAuth scopes are not enforced, and #4415 made the OAuth API live.** `OAuthInterceptor.enforceScope` (`:284-287`) returns at once unless `oauth.scope.enforcement.enabled` is set, and no shipped `carlos.properties` sets it. An app the provider approves for one listed scope can call every `/ws/services` read and write the provider can | Source. Fix: ship the property as true, or treat its absence as enabled Filed as #4419. Fixed on release/2026.08 by #4461 (a token is held to the scopes the provider approved). Re-verified live on the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package (release/2026.08 at 0c43d2f862): the step that was pinned (a token approved for demographic.read is refused at /ws/services/allergies/active with the application's 403 and no data) passes; the release's own `oauth-rest-surfaces` now covers the access modes. | `fixed` |
| 151 | **Prescription lists now show every dated copy of a drug.** #4286 keys `RxPrescriptionData.getUniquePrescriptionsByPatient` (`:519-575`) on the whole record, start, end and written dates included, where `main` kept one entry per product. A drug prescribed again by search rather than ReRx appears once per prescription in the E-Chart Medications panel, the consultation "Current Medications" list, chart print and flowsheets, expired copies included; the REST view still shows one per product | Source; the commit's own unit test asserts both copies are kept. Fix: merge on product and regimen, not on the dated history fields Filed as #4420. Fixed on release/2026.08 by #4449. Live on the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package (release/2026.08 at 0c43d2f862), with `CONSULTATION_AUTO_INCLUDE_MEDICATIONS=true` set and the app restarted: `rx-unique-medication-list` passes all four steps. #4420 closed. | `fixed` |
| 152 | **The Rourke and BCAR measurement modal saves the UTC date.** `form/demographicMeasurementModal.jsp:175` defaults the observation date to `new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10)`, and `:219` lists stored dates the same way. After 17:00 in BC (20:00 in Ontario, daylight time) the modal saves tomorrow's date. #4292 made Save work | Source: the lines cited. Live: `form-rourke2020-growth` (2026-10-09, packaged install, browser zone Pacific/Pago_Pago so that the clinician's date and the UTC date differ): the Rourke 2020 dialog offered 2026-10-09 and filed a weight on 2026-10-09 on a day that is 2026-10-08 where the clinician is. The BCAR half is not driven (BC is out of scope). Pinned by `form-rourke2020-growth-measure-date`. Fix: format the local date Filed as #4421. Fixed on release/2026.08 by #4443 (the dialog defaults to the clinician's local date). Re-verified live on the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package (release/2026.08 at 0c43d2f862): `form-rourke2020-growth-measure-date` passes in the browser time zone that exposed it. | `fixed` |
| 153 | **Saving a BC bill whose physician is no longer listed blanks its provider.** The adjust page's physician select (`adjustBill.jsp:669-684`) lists only active providers with a billing number, under a blank first option. For any other bill nothing is selected, and `BillingReProcessBill2Action` (`:189-202`, `:436`) writes the empty value to `billing.provider_no` and the billingmaster payee and practitioner numbers. Made reachable by #4373 | Source. Fix: keep the stored provider when none is posted, and list the bill's own provider Filed as #4422. Pinned by `billing-bc-reprocess-bill-inactive-physician` (BC_REPROCESS_PIN=inactive). Live on a BC install (release build 1aee7c707a): with the bill's physician inactive, the adjust page's physician select offered `""`, and after Reprocess Bill all four columns read `""`. | `issue-filed` |
| 154 | **Dashboard Assign Tickler rejects ordinary dates and reports it as an uncertain save.** `TicklerRequest` (#4320) parses the service date and time strictly as `MM-dd-yyyy hh:mm a`. The modal's date picker never initializes (its inline script is removed when the modal body is sanitized), and its configured format is `Y-m-d`, which the server would also refuse. An ISO date gets HTTP 400 and the alert "The tickler save could not be confirmed" | Source. Fix: accept the picker's format, and return and show the validation reason Filed as #4423. | `issue-filed` |
| 155 | **"rev" on a group note answers a blank 403.** #4307 refuses note history unless the note's own `demographic_no` equals the chart's (`CaseManagementEntry2Action.java:2838`); a group note shown read-only in a linked client's chart belongs to the other client | Source. Filed as #4545. **Fixed (#4545, PR #4694):** history follows the modern chart's recipient clinical-read and active-sharing authority, with individual patient access, recipient program-domain and enabled-facility checks. Only explicitly linked revisions with the same owner and UUID, at or before the selected revision, are exposed; cumulative history is stripped from request-owned copies. Legacy group-note display uses copies and avoids unnecessary owner-record hydration. On 2026-10-15 all six native browser steps pass: actual Rev, a permitted different-role reader, private/future revision refusal, restricted and inactive-only clinical-grant refusal, and link revocation, with both patients and clinical rows unchanged. The 275-check Java selection includes actual active-role/privilege DAO authorization and real Hibernate/H2 persistence. | fixed |
| 156 | **Patient photo upload and Clear Photo act on whichever photo popup was opened last.** `ClientImage2Action` (`:88`, `:151`) reads the patient from the session-wide `clientId`. With two patients' photo popups open, Clear Photo in the first deletes the second patient's photo. #4308 made Clear Photo work | Source. Fix: post the patient number from the form and check access to that patient. Filed as #4546. **Fixed (2026-10-14, #4546):** both forms of `uploadimage.jsp` post the popup's `demographicNo` and the page no longer writes `clientId`; `ClientImage2Action` acts only on exactly one posted positive patient number (no session fallback) and checks `_demographic` w for that patient and `isAllowedAccessToPatientRecord` before reading or writing the photo. On 2026-10-14 `patient-photo-upload` passed on a `2026.09.0~snapshot26` package built from the fix branch and installed into Ubuntu 26.04, including the step above, Upload in the first manager, a POST that names no patient, and a doctor login locked out of the patient (refused, photo kept; the unlocked control clears). With the pre-fix class and JSP swapped into that install, Clear Photo in the first manager again deleted the second patient's photo and the locked login was served. The expected-failure pin is removed. | fixed |
| 157 | **The Disease Registry report adds a whole quick list when one code is added.** #4331 bound the select as `quickListName`; a list preselected through `${quickLists.lastUsed}` (`oscarReportDxReg.jsp:176`), which another page's session handler marks, is posted with every single-code Add | Source: `dxSetupResearch2Action:113` replaces the session's `allQuickLists` with a handler that marks a list selected, and `addSearchCode` does not reset it. Filed as #4547. Fixed by #4656: the report builds its own unselected handler as the request attribute `dxReportQuickLists` for every render (`DxresearchReport2Action.REPORT_QUICK_LISTS`), no longer reads or replaces `allQuickLists`, and renders the selector without a preselection. Live on the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package (release/2026.08 at e9ce38de): `dx-registry-status-update` fails at its new step "a quick list the patient registry marks is never preselected in the report" (`After adding 401 the report preselected quick list "List1" (registry marked "List1")`). The seeded `List1` adds no codes (its rows use coding system `ichppc`, which no lookup recognises), so a probe made the owned list the registry's marked list: one single-code Add of 272 then added 250, 272, 401 and the list's `ichppccode` code. On the fix's package (2026.09.0~snapshot26+issue4547r2) that step passes, including its check that a list created while the report is open appears on the next render; so do the probe, `dx-registry-quicklist`, `popup-opener-dx-registry-back` and `export-content-dx-registry-xlsx`. Before the fix these fail: `DxresearchReport2ActionTest` `shouldRenderUnselectedReportQuickLists_whenReportOpens`, `shouldListCurrentQuickLists_whenReportRerendersAfterAdd` and `shouldLeaveRegistryQuickListsUntouched_whenReportOpens`, and both `OscarReportDxRegJspRegressionTest` tests. | `fixed` |
| 158 | **Note history ignores the per-patient chart lock.** `notehistory` checks `_demographic` and `_casemgmt.notes` for the patient but not `isAllowedAccessToPatientRecord`, so a role locked out of a chart with `_eChart$<demo>` still reads its notes' revisions. Predates #4307 | Source. Fix: add the record-access check, as in finding 60's routes. Authorization defect for a signed-in user: tracked in umbrella [#4223](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/4223), section A. | issue-filed |
| 159 | **An additional document review can be recorded in another provider's name.** `AddEditDocument2Action` (`:717`) stores the posted `extraReviewerId`. #4293 took the first review's author from the session but left this path | Source. Fix: use the logged-in provider. Authorization defect for a signed-in user: tracked in umbrella [#4223](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/4223), section A. | issue-filed |
| 160 | **The CDS import lock can be dropped mid-import.** `CdsImportLock` (#4314) holds `GET_LOCK` on a pooled connection that sits idle during the import, while `carlos.properties` ships `db_remove_abandoned=true` with a 15-second timeout. Under pool pressure DBCP reclaims it, releasing the lock, and the final `RELEASE_LOCK` then throws after the result was written, so a completed import is reported as failed | Source. Fix: hold the lock on a connection outside abandoned-connection tracking, and log rather than throw on release. Filed as #4548. | issue-filed |
| 161 | **The CDM report omits measurements entered on its end date.** `MeasurementDaoImpl.findByCreateDate` (`:762-764`) compares the `createDate` timestamp with `<=` the end date at midnight; the end date defaults to today. #4309 restored the counts | Source. Fix: an exclusive bound of the day after. Filed as #4549. Fixed for #4549: `MeasurementDao.findByCreateDate(from, toExclusive)` is half-open and `RptMeasurementsData` passes midnight of the day after the end date. | fixed |
| 162 | **Unlock Account shows site-restricted administrators nothing in the packaged lock mode.** #4353 filters the lock list by usernames (`UnLock2Action.java:85,110`), but with `login_lock` unset the keys are client addresses, so the list is always empty and a direct unlock is refused | Source. Fixed: `UnLock2Action` applies the site filter only when `login_lock=true`; in the default address-keyed mode it lists and unlocks every tracked address. Pinned by `UnLock2ActionUnitTest` (`shouldListEveryAddress_whenSitePrivacyAppliesButLocksAreKeyedByAddress`, `shouldUnlockAndAuditAddress_whenSitePrivacyAppliesButLocksAreKeyedByAddress`) and by the manual `account-unlock-address-mode` browser check, run against a packaged install with `login_lock` unset. Filed as #4550. | fixed |
| 163 | **Administration ▸ Key Manager ▸ Create Key creates a key pair on GET.** `ViewKeygenCreateKey2Action` checks `_admin` w but not the method, and `createKey.jsp:55-76` calls `KeyPairGen.createKeys` for any request with a name, so a cross-site GET registers keys | Source. Fix: refuse non-POST when a name is present, as the sibling update action does. Listed in #3059 (child #2864, closed as a duplicate of it). | issue-filed |
| 164 | **BC Receive Payment still saves nothing.** `receivePayment.jsp` reads a `receivePaymentActionForm` attribute no action sets, so its hidden `billingmasterNo` and `billNo` inputs are empty and the Method list has no options; a submit archives no payment and shows the empty form again. The #4373 DAO fix is needed but not enough | Source: no Java sets the attribute (`git grep receivePaymentActionForm -- src/main/java` is empty); `receivePayment.jsp:36-56`. Filed as #4551. Fixed in #4551: both actions now publish themselves as `receivePaymentActionForm`, the hidden inputs carry their values, `ReceivePayment2Action` is POST-only and validates the submission, and a malformed submit re-renders the form with an error. Pinned by `billing-bc-receive-payment` and `ReceivePayment2ActionUnitTest`. | fixed |
| 165 | **A PathNet report mixing final and pending results is listed as Final.** The report status is the alphabetical minimum of its OBR statuses (`PathnetResultsData.java:188-189`), so F with I or P reads F. #4356 made these labs list again | Source: `Hl7MshDao` selects `MIN(obr.resultStatus)`, and `DemographicLab.jsp:315` shows Final for F. Filed as #4552. **Fixed (#4552):** the report status aggregate (`PathNetLabResults.REPORT_STATUS_HQL`, used by `Hl7MshDao` and `Hl7ObrDao`) keeps a single shared status but reports a report that mixes statuses as `P`, so F with I or P is listed Partial. `pathnet-status` pins it. Verified live on a BC install (2026.09.0~snapshot26 package built from the fix branch, installed fresh with the demo dataset into an Ubuntu 26.04 container, `carlos-ctl check` clean, `PATHNET_LABS=yes`): all four steps pass, and with the pre-fix `Hl7MshDao` and `Hl7ObrDao` classes swapped into the installed webapp the check FAILS at "a PathNet report with both final and preliminary results is listed as Partial, not Final" (listed as Final). | fixed |
| 166 | **The Framingham/UKPDS calculator accepts HDL above total cholesterol.** #4398's range checks are per field (`riskcalc/js/js.js:28-29`); swapped boxes give a confidently low risk | Source. Fix: refuse HDL at or above total cholesterol. Filed as #4553. | issue-filed |
| 167 | **Rx drug information shows nothing for a drug whose DIN DrugRef does not know.** #4288's links send both the name and the DIN; an unresolved DIN yields an empty monograph without the name search (`RxDrugInfo2Action.java:76-88`) | Source: the Info links in `StaticScript2.jsp:381`, `ViewScript2.jsp:1761` and `WriteScript.jsp:1575` send both parameters. Filed as #4554. Fixed by #4705: a missing or empty exact monograph falls back to name-search results, and an exact lookup that succeeds still comes first. A live check on 2026-10-16 (CARLOS built from #4705 on Tomcat 11 with MariaDB 11.8 and the dev DrugRef data, not a packaged install) found that the description the Info link sends, such as `ATORVASTATIN (ATORVASTATIN CALCIUM) 20.0mg tablet`, matches nothing in the catalogue search, so an unknown DIN still ended at "no matches". The fallback now also searches the description's name part (the words before its strength), and `gap-encounter-rx-drug-info-on-host` pins that in its last step, which passes there. Not yet re-verified on a packaged install. | `fixed` |
| 168 | **A failure after an eForm is stored is reported as a duplicate submission.** #4211's `AddEForm2Action` (`:165-171`) answers every post-commit `RuntimeException` with the replay 409, "This form can no longer be submitted from this page". So when Add to documents fails (for example `DocumentManagerImpl.moveDocument` requires `_edoc` x, `:406`), or a chart-note, tickler, fax or email step does, the clinician is told nothing failed, and the log keeps only the exception's class name | Source: the catch returns `rejectSubmission(false)` with the same text as a replayed token. Fix: a distinct "saved, but <step> did not complete" message, keeping the token consumed Filed as #4424. **Fixed (#4424):** a failure after the storage transaction started keeps the token spent and answers HTTP 409 with its own text, never the replay refusal. After a confirmed commit `eform.submitSavedStepIncomplete` names the step, so a refused Add to Documents reads "The eForm was saved, but Add to Documents did not complete."; the other steps are the template's chart entries, the fax, download and email preparation, and "a follow-on step". A commit whose outcome is unknown answers `eform.submitOutcomeUnknown` ("CARLOS could not confirm whether this eForm was saved."). The log records the step and `LogSafe.exceptionTrace` (exception types and frames; no message, no patient or eForm id). Covered by `AddEForm2ActionPostSaveFailureUnitTest` and `EFormPostSaveFailureMessageUnitTest`; the `double-submit-eform` step "a failed Add to Documents is not answered with the replay refusal" is no longer an expected failure, but has not yet been re-run live on a package. | `fixed` |
| 169 | **The eForm 409 message drops its apostrophe.** `getText` formats `eform.submitUnavailable` through `MessageFormat`, which treats the single quote in "patient's" as quoting, so users read "Check the patients eForms"; the same text is in all five locale files | Live: a second POST of one eForm submission token answered 409 "... Check the patients eForms before reopening the form ...". Fix: `patient''s` in each locale file Filed as #4424. | `issue-filed` |
| 170 | **Delete Style Sheet never deletes a style sheet.** `EctDeleteMeasurementStyleSheet2Action` (`:75-97`) removes only group mappings whose style sheet is already gone and refuses when a group uses it; an unused style sheet stays listed after Delete, with no message. The JPA conversion lost the original delete, and #4352's new tests cover only the orphan-mapping case | Source: no `MeasurementCSSLocation` remove on any path. Fix: remove the style sheet when no group maps it. Filed as #4555. **Fixed (#4555):** the action now removes the `MeasurementCSSLocation` row (and its uploaded file) when no group maps it, and refuses the whole request, deleting nothing, if any selected sheet is still in use. Covered by unit and integration tests; not yet re-verified live on a package. | `fixed` |

### Found by the server-log triage and the follow-up review

From the server-log triage of the Ontario suite run and the review's second pass. Each row was
checked in the source at 9784de2710 before it was filed.

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 171 | **Inbox "add queue" creates a queue on GET with only `_edoc` read.** `DmsInboxManage2Action.execute()` (`:108-125`) checks `_edoc` r and dispatches `method=addNewQueue` to `addNewQueue()` (`:665-676`), which creates the queue and its security object on any method. #2655 moved `updateDocStatusInQueue` ahead of the read gate with its own write check and left this one. A missing `newQueueName` also NPEs (`trim()` before the null check) | Live: `get-reject-inbox-queue` fails; its HEAD replay logged `QueueDaoImpl:129` and `DmsInboxManage2Action:676`. Filed as #4428. Authorization defect for a signed-in user: tracked in umbrella [#4223](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/4223), section A. Open PR #4445 fixes it (requires POST and `_edoc` write). | `issue-filed` |
| 172 | **`/ws/services` is not rate-limited, and every rejected call writes a synchronous audit row.** The packaged nginx (`debian/assets/nginx/carlos-emr.conf:205-262`) limits only login, reset and MFA, and `/ws/LoginService` and `/ws/oauth/initiate|token`; `OAuthInterceptor.auditAuthFailure` (`:259-270`) calls `LogAction.addLogSynchronous` for each rejection. #4415 made the surface live | Source. Filed as #4429. Fix merged on release/2026.08 in #4461; the release's `oauth-rest-surfaces` (flood steps) passes against the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package (release/2026.08 at 0c43d2f862). Open PR #4441 adds the nginx front-door limit and bounds the failure audit rows (closes #4429); the front-door pin is the `todo` test `shouldRateLimitWsServices_atTheFrontDoor` in `scripts/nginx-rate-limit.test.js`. | `issue-filed` |
| 173 | **The Ontario RA payment report skips RAs paid on the last day of the month.** `BillingOnPaymentViewModelAssembler` (`:206-221`) ends the window on the month's last day, and both `RaDetailDaoImpl.getRaDetailByDate` overloads (`:141-170`) compare `paymentDate < end` | Source: the bound is exclusive and `paymentDate` is a `yyyyMMdd` string. Filed as #4430. Fixed on release/2026.08 by #4442 (the report includes RAs paid on the month's last day). Re-verified live on the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package (release/2026.08 at 0c43d2f862): `billing-on-premium-payment-date` passes, including the RA paid on the 30th. | `fixed` |
| 174 | **The DrugRef `postinst` recovery hint cannot work.** The two exit-1 paths (`debian/carlos-emr-drugref.postinst:117-133`) tell the administrator to run `dpkg-reconfigure carlos-emr-drugref`, which dpkg refuses for the half-configured package the failed `postinst` leaves | Source: both paths `exit 1` after printing the hint. Filed as #4431. | `issue-filed` |
| 175 | **The DrugRef `postinst` provisions the database while an OSCAR 19 import is paused.** `acquire_provision_lock` (`:31-45`) consults the import guard only when the lock is busy; a paused import holds no lock, but its ledger still reads "in progress" to `carlos-emr-o19-guard`, so `db-users` and the grants run | Source: `flock -n 9 && return 0` precedes the guard check. Filed as #4432. | `issue-filed` |
| 176 | **Customized flowsheets lose their flowsheet-level decision-support rules.** `makeNewFlowsheet` (`:1488`, from `:1287`) re-parses the output of `getExportFlowsheet` (`:1659`), which does not write the root `ds_rules`, so `createflowsheet` (`:957-958`) never loads the DRL file. This affects diabetes, hypertension, HIV, asthma, COPD, heart failure and the health tracker. `getMessages` then throws `IllegalStateException`, blamed on compilation | Live: `HealthTrackerPage_jspf:652` and `TemplateFlowSheetPage_jspf:387` `getMessages` ERRORs during the flowsheet customization checks; source for the cause. Filed as #4433. Fixed on release/2026.08 by #4459 (customized flowsheets keep the flowsheet-level decision-support rules). Re-verified live on the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package (release/2026.08 at 0c43d2f862): `flowsheet-patient-customization` passes, including the CKD warning step. | `fixed` |
| 177 | **The eForm editor's letterhead uses AP keys `apconfig.xml` does not define.** `editControl2.js:1122-1123,1490-1496` offers and uses `doctor_contact_phone`, `doctor_contact_fax` and `doctor_contact_addr`; each lookup fails and the letterhead renders them blank | Live: four `efmformapconfig_005flookup_jsp:335` ERRORs during the eForm render checks; `apconfig.xml` defines the `clinic_*` and `doctor` keys but none of these. Filed as #4434. Issue #4434 is closed, but its fix, #4454, merged to `main` only: `release/2026.08` at `501317e153` still offers the `doctor_contact_*` keys. | `issue-filed` |
| 178 | **An allergy can be saved without its drug identifiers.** `RxAddAllergy2Action` (`:173-182`) catches the `NoSuchElementException` from `RxDrugData.getDrug` and saves the allergy without its regional identifier and ATC code, which, per `RxDrugData.java:654-663`, silently disables drug-allergy checks for it. Whether DrugRef data or the search's id type is at fault is open | Live: two `RxAddAllergy2Action:180` ERRORs for DrugRef id 39007 while `allergy-add-penicillin` and `allergy-injected-form-csrf` added a penicillin allergy. Filed as #4435. The pin in `allergy-add-penicillin` (2026-10-08, packaged install) finds the added allergy and its amendment stored with an empty `regional_identifier` and `atc` and no `RxAddAllergy2Action` ERROR in the run's journal window: the Penicillin shortcut's id 44452 is a real DrugRef row (finding 215), so the lookup returns a record without the class's identifiers instead of throwing. Re-checked live on the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package (release/2026.08 at 0c43d2f862) after #4446 and #4455 merged: still reproduces (`allergy-add-penicillin` fails at its pinned step). **Fixed (2026-10-15, #4576, with finding 215):** the Penicillin shortcut now records an ATC class allergen: TYPECODE 8, the class code J01C in `atc`, and no DrugRef id. A class has no DIN, so `regional_identifier` stays empty by design (it is exported as a DIN). The last step of `allergy-add-penicillin` now requires the class code and no DIN on both rows, and no `RxAddAllergy2Action` ERROR in the run's journal window; its expectedFailure is removed. Live on a 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package built from the fix branch and installed into an Ubuntu 26.04 container (`carlos-ctl check` clean, `:443`): the check passes. | `fixed` |
| 179 | **A failed lab upload logs HHH000099 instead of its real error.** `MessageUploader.clean` (`:642-644`) queries the session the failed insert poisoned, so the upload actions log Hibernate's assertion; the database error appears only at WARN | Live: the database-trigger probes of `lab-upload-rollback` and `lab-upload-signed-feed` logged the trigger at WARN, then `AssertionFailure` and `InsideLabUpload2Action:205` at ERROR. Filed as #4436. Fixed on release/2026.08 by #4456. Live on the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package (release/2026.08 at 0c43d2f862): `lab-upload-rollback` with `LAB_UPLOAD_JOURNAL_UNIT=carlos-emr` passes, so the database's own error is logged at ERROR and no `HHH000099` appears. #4436 closed. | `fixed` |
| 180 | **FaxImporter logs an ERROR on every poll for a failing account, with no backoff.** `FaxImporter.java:405-407` logs each provider failure and the scheduler retries every minute indefinitely; nothing is surfaced to the administrator | Live: 189 identical `FaxImporter.java:406` ERRORs, one a minute, across three hours and a restart (the `fax-configure` check's unreachable account). Filed as #4437. | `issue-filed` |
| 181 | **Client aborts and expected 401s are logged at ERROR.** `DbConnectionFilter` (`:94-96`) and `ResponseSanitizationFilter` (`:338-342`) both log a `ClientAbortException` at ERROR, and `ResponseSanitizationFilter` (`:390-395`) logs every sanitized error body at ERROR, including the expected `/ws/services` 401s | Live: abort pairs on font, script and image downloads, and `ResponseSanitizationFilter:393` ERRORs for anonymous `/ws/services` calls. Filed as #4438. Fix merged on release/2026.08 in #4461; its anonymous-refusal half is covered by the release's `oauth-rest-surfaces`, which passes against the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package (release/2026.08 at 0c43d2f862); the client-abort half was checked on 2026-10-09: the installed filters use `ClientAbort`, 25 downloads aborted mid-transfer against Tomcat logged no ERROR or WARN, and the journal holds 15 such ERRORs before the 13:52 upgrade and none after. #4438 closed. | `fixed` |

### Found by the 2026.08 coverage mapping

From mapping the Ontario coverage gaps for the 2026.08 Playwright plan
([the plan](../superpowers/plans/2026-10-08-playwright-coverage-gaps.md)). Each row was checked in
the source at a7da4f23ee before it was filed. "Live" marks one also reproduced on the packaged
Ontario install (`carlos-emr 2026.08.0~alpha19`, demo data) with owned FAKE patients; where a
probe had to change clinic-wide state (a role's privilege on an object, one form registration), the
row was restored to its seeded value afterwards. The British Columbia candidates from the same
mapping are tracked in [#4439](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/4439) and are not
logged here.

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 182 | **Two charts open in one login cross over: a chart window's navbar modules, measurement history and form setup follow whichever patient was opened last.** The chart keeps ONE session-wide `EctSessionBean`. `EctDisplayAction` (`:169-178`) rebuilds it only when the session has no bean yet or the request names `demographicNo` (`:170`); the module loaders (`/encounter/displayForms`, `displayLabs` and the rest) are requested without one, so they use the bean. `EctSetupDisplayHistory2Action` (`:62-70`) and `EctSetupHistoryIndex2Action` (`:63-68`) read the bean alone, `FrmSetupForm2Action` (`:116-118`) replaces the request's own `demographic_no` with the bean's whenever a bean exists (its test is `demo == null` or `bean != null`, and the second term is true for any session that has opened a chart), and `FrmForm2Action` (`:196`) saves the form to the bean's patient. A history, form or module opened from patient A's window shows or acts on patient B. The same bean decides what the history's Plot control draws (`DisplayHistory.jsp:284` takes the patient from the history page's bean-derived `demo`), which patient the Measurements "+" Vitals entry popup and the Preventions "+" popup are opened for once their module has been repainted from the bean (`EctDisplayMeasurements2Action` and `EctDisplayPrevention2Action` build those links from `bean.demographicNo`), and whose rows the history's Delete control can select. Because any request that names `demographicNo` rebuilds the bean (`EctDisplayAction:170`), which patient a window follows depends on which window made the last such request, so the crossover is order-dependent and can flip back. The Rx module's own single session-wide patient bean had the same fault (opening a second patient's Rx switched the first tab's stash) and was fixed per tab in #3908 | Live: two owned FAKE patients A and B, A's chart open, then B's chart in a second window of the same login. (1) A's window, Measurements module, BP history link: A's seeded value before B's chart opened, B's value after, same link and window. (2) A's window, Forms module reloaded with `reloadNav('forms')` (what a closing popup does): every Add Form link carried B's demographic number, and Rourke2017 opened from it for B with B's name on the page. (3) The Vascular Tracker, the only form routed through `SetupForm`, opened from A's window with `demographic_no` set to A, was headed with A's name before B's chart opened and B's name after (its page prints the bean). Not drivable as seeded: the registration `SetupForm.do?formName=VTForm` answers 404 from the menu and is refused by `SetupForm` itself (HTTP 400, `isSetupFormEndpoint` rejects the `.do`), so (3) ran with that one `encounterForm` row corrected and then restored; `SubmitForm` answered 400 to both the page's Update button and a direct post, so the save in `FrmForm2Action` is Source only. (4) The `echart-two-patient-isolation` check, A's window with B's chart open: the WT history opened from the Measurements module listed only B's measurement and was headed with B's name; the history's Plot control drew a different PNG from the one A's own history drew before B opened (B's data); the reloaded Measurements module showed B's weight and not A's; the Vitals entry popup opened from A's window (Measurements "+" menu, after the module had been repainted) was addressed `SetupMeasurements?demographicNo=` with B's number; the Preventions "+" after its list popup had closed and repainted the module opened `ViewPreventionIndex` for B; and the history that carries the Delete control listed only B's reading, so a Delete from A's window can select only B's rows (no Delete was submitted on the buggy build). (5) Order dependence, re-run on 2026-10-09 with a probe on the check's own fixtures (packaged install through `:443`; B's chart loaded last, then in A's window a CPP item saved, then the Preventions list popup opened and closed so the module repainted): the popup opened from A's window opened for A right after the CPP save (that save reloads the CPP boxes with `demographicNo`) and again after the repaint, and when B's chart was loaded again the first popup still used the link rendered for A and, after the module repainted, the next one opened for B; the Dx "+" popup and the CPP "+" kept A throughout, because their links name A and were not rebuilt from the bean. Filed as #4556. Fix verified for #4556 in PR #4667: explicit per-window patient context, patient-scoped encounter metadata, form ownership and measurement write/delete/graph checks. The expanded actual-app Ontario and BC regressions each pass all 23 steps, including reciprocal clinical writes, invalid/foreign identity rejection, VT Update/reopen/Save and Exit and opener navigation; cleanup restores each complete form registry and leaves zero owned clinical rows. The original release check reproduces the crossover as a negative control. | `fixed` |
| 183 | **Every link the Pregnancy module's heading, Add menu and current-episode item render goes to a route that does not exist.** `EctDisplayPregnancy2Action` (`:65-66`, `:81-87`, `:109`) builds `/Pregnancy?method=list` (the heading), `create` (the four Add menu items) and `complete` (the current-episode item), and assigns an `edit` URL (`:66`) it never renders, but no Struts action named `Pregnancy` is mapped in any `struts-*.xml` (only `encounter/displayPregnancies`), so `commn/web/Pregnancy2Action` and its methods cannot be reached. The seed hides the module from the stock `doctor` role (a bare `o` on `_pregnancy` and `_newCasemgmt.pregnancy`), so the dead links appear only once a clinic grants read. Mapping the action alone would not finish the job: `HttpMethodGuardFilter` already answers a GET with `method=create` or `method=complete` 405, and the module opens both as GET popups | Live: with the `doctor` role's two privileges raised from `o` to `r` (restored afterwards), the chart's Pregnancies heading link opened a popup that showed the CARLOS 404 page; a direct GET of `/Pregnancy?method=list&demographicNo=<owned patient>` answered 404 `text/html` (the 2,524-byte error page), `method=edit` 404, and `method=create` and `method=complete` 405 with `Allow: POST`, identical to a nonexistent action name (`/NoSuchActionXyz?method=create` is also 405). `GET /encounter/displayPregnancies`, the module loader, answered 200. Source: `git grep Pregnancy -- 'src/main/webapp/WEB-INF/classes/*.xml'` finds only the loader. Filed as #4557. **Fixed (#4557, PR #4694):** the mapped, allowlisted Pregnancy action serves read-only list/new/edit pages and CSRF-protected POST create/complete forms. The heading, all four Add choices, current episode and completed history work for the explicit patient. Patient/episode ownership, individual restrictions, read/write privileges, valid dates and serialized atomic mutations are enforced; unreviewed legacy helpers remain unreachable. On 2026-10-15 all eight native browser steps pass, including literal-note round trips, safe reload, own-chart refresh, GET refusal, invalid/stale requests and foreign/read-only/restricted denial without demographic or episode changes. The focused selection passes 65 Java checks, including six real H2 transaction/concurrency cases, and 16 JavaScript checks. | fixed |
| 184 | **Forward on the MDS, CML and PathNet lab pages and on the patient lab list opens a provider picker that cannot be submitted, and the form behind it would route nothing.** `oscarMDS/SelectProvider.jsp` shows the provider search, Forward List and Favorites, but its Submit button is commented out (`:169`) and nothing else calls `prepSubmit()` (`:58`), so a chosen provider never reaches the opener's form. Restoring the button would not be enough: `prepSubmit` looks the opener's form up as `reassignForm_<docId>` (`:87`, `:90`, `:104`), while `CMLDisplay.jsp:148` and `DemographicLab.jsp:185` name theirs `reassignForm`, so for those callers (no `docId`) the lookup finds nothing and the popup closes without sending. Fixing both would still route nothing: those pages' own `reassignForm` posts `flaggedLabs` as the bare lab number and `selectedProviders` as a bare provider list, `ReportReassign2Action` (`:122-146`) parses both as JSON objects (`{"files":[...]}`, `{"providers":[...]}`) and routes nothing when they do not parse, and for a non-AJAX post it then redirects to the request path with `&providerNo=...` appended and no `?` (`:209-221`), a path that is not a route. The live callers are `CMLDisplay.jsp:192` and `:628`, `SegmentDisplay.jsp:203` and `:855`, the BC `labDisplay.jsp:157` and `:611` and `DemographicLab.jsp:134` (`oscarMDS/documentsInQueues.jsp:1769` also opens the picker from `checkSelected()`, which that file defines at `:1752` and never calls); the Inbox list view is not affected (it opens the same page in its own dialog with a Forward button, `oscarMDSIndex.js:1593-1650`) | Live: for the popup page only, `GET /oscarMDS/ViewSelectProvider` (with and without `docId` and `labDisplay`) answered 200 and its controls were the search box, the Forward List and Favorites lists and the `>>` and `<<` buttons; there was no submit control, `#submitButton` did not exist and no element called `prepSubmit`. Source: the callers and the form-name mismatch. Then driven from the CML lab page with an owned CML lab (the `legacy-lab-display` check, which fails there): Forward opens the picker, the provider search offers the recipient, the Forward List takes it, and the picker's controls are `#forwardList`, `#autocompleteprov`, `#fwdProviders`, the `>>` and `<<` buttons and `#favorites`, with no submit control; the PathNet page's picker is the same. A probe that submitted the CML page's own `reassignForm` with a provider number in `selectedProviders` (what `prepSubmit` would send if it were reached) was answered 302 to `/carlos/lab/CA/ON/Forward&providerNo=999998&searchProviderNo=&status=`, ended on the application's public landing page, created no routing row, and the journal logged `Failed to parse selectedProviders JSON` and `Failed to parse flaggedLabs JSON` (`IntNode cannot be cast to ObjectNode`). The MDS page's picker was driven once finding 194 was fixed (#4654, 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package): it is the same picker, it offers the recipient and takes it into the Forward List, and it has no submit control, so `legacy-lab-display-mds` now stops at its Forward step on this finding. Filed as #4558. **Fixed on release/2026.08 (#4558).** The picker has a Forward button (`#submitButton`; popup only, the Inbox dialog keeps its own) that posts the opener's `reassignForm` selection, the Forward List and the Favorites list in the Inbox's JSON contract, from a form built in the opener's document (so its CSRFGuard client adds the token and the redirect loads there); it says why and stays open when no lab page is behind it, no lab is selected or the Forward List is empty. The patient lab list's checkboxes carry `data-lab-type`. `ReportReassign2Action` validates the whole request before any write and answers 400 otherwise, keeps one array per field (before, a provider list that did not parse left the favourites in the shared array and the lab was routed to them), leaves the stored favourites alone when no list is posted (before, every such post deleted them all), refuses non-POST, and redirects a page post to the posting page's own display route, or to the patient lab list, from a fixed table. The dead `checkSelected()` in `documentsInQueues.jsp` is removed. Re-verified on the `2026.09.0~snapshot26` package built from the fix branch (Ubuntu 26.04 container, `carlos-ctl check` clean): `legacy-lab-display` and `legacy-lab-display-mds` pass every step with their `expectedFailure` removed, and the new `lab-forward-picker` passes (the picker's refusals, the 400 for the pre-fix payload, and Forward from the patient lab list); with the pre-fix action and JSPs swapped in, `legacy-lab-display` fails at its Forward step again; with only the pre-fix action, the Forward is redirected to `/carlos/lab/CA/ON/Forward&providerNo=999998&searchProviderNo=&status=&segmentID=<id>%3ACML` and the bare-id post is answered 302. | `fixed` |

Replayed and pinned by `scripts/demo-dataset-contract.test.js` (`shouldMatchSecObjPrivilege_acrossSeedsAndDemoData`: five `todo` subtests, each with a second subtest that fails on any further drift). The packaged install and its additive demo artifact agree with the seeds. Filed as #4566. The doctor-role differences for `_eform`, `_episode`, `_newCasemgmt.episode` and `_admin.flowsheet` remained. **Fixed (2026-10-14, #4566):** the devcontainer now follows the seeds for all four. `development_privileges.sql` sets doctor `_eform` `w`, `_admin.flowsheet` `r`, and `_episode` and `_newCasemgmt.episode` `o`. The Episodes `x` was a hand edit on the demo server: the snapshot's recyclebin rows 5 and 7 (2023-10-06) record the earlier `o`, and granting the panel to doctors would need its own migration. `seed_data.sh` re-runs the file, so existing volumes are repaired on rebuild, and `/resetsql-developer` now re-applies the security migrations before it. The contract test's replay now runs `reapply_security_migrations.sh` itself, which it had not modelled; that is why `_admin.fieldnote` still read as different after #4452. Its five `todo` subtests pass and are removed. A new subtest requires the devcontainer's whole `secObjPrivilege` table to equal the Ontario seed. With the seeds' `o` now in both builds, `episode-lifecycle` and `record-access` grant both objects to the test login's provider number for their run (`scripts/lib/episode-module-access.js`) instead of relying on the devcontainer's `x`. The module needs both: `EctDisplayAction` checks `_episode` (`"_" + cmd`, through `SecurityManager.hasReadAccess`) before `EctDisplayEpisode2Action` checks `_newCasemgmt.episode`. `episode-lifecycle` first asserts the module stays hidden without them. On the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package both checks now pass with no hand-granted privilege. On a fresh MariaDB 11.8 load through `populate_db.sh`, the devcontainer table equals a Flyway-only load row for row. | fixed |
| 194 | **The MDS lab page throws an error on every MDS lab.** `MdsMSHDaoImpl.findMdsSementDataById` (`:60-69`) builds its HQL from `"AND mdsMSH.id = :segmentID" + "GROUP BY mdsMSH.id"`, which joins the parameter name and `GROUP BY` into `:segmentIDGROUP BY`, and then binds a parameter named `id`, so the query never parses. `MDSSegmentData.populateMDSSegmentData` (`:217`) catches and logs the exception and leaves `providers`, the report date, status, client number and accession unset, and `SegmentDisplay.jsp:451` dereferences `mDSSegmentData.providers.referringDoctor` (the Requesting Client cell) and throws a `NullPointerException`; the CARLOS error block is then printed in the middle of the report with HTTP 200, so a clinician gets the patient block and an error where the results should be. CARLOS Error: 500`, no results table, no report date or accession. Acknowledge from the same page wrote status A and the comment. Pinned by `legacy-lab-display-mds` at its step "MDS display shows the lab's values and its patient". The header query is fixed, and so is everything behind it, each reproduced against H2 with the production table shape (the message date cast, `em.find` on multi-row `mdsZMN`, the notes lookup and key types, the report-group HQL and its `count()` cast, `findResultCodes`, the OBR casts, and `GroupedReports.resultsArray`, which was never initialised). The most serious was found then: the result query returned `MdsOBX` entities keyed by the `segmentID` every row of a report shares, so a rendering report would have shown its first result once per row. Re-verified live on the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package built from that branch (Ubuntu 26.04 container, `carlos-ctl check` clean): `legacy-lab-display-mds`, now with two results and a note in one report, passes display, acknowledge and the picker and stops at Forward on finding 184; with the pre-fix classes and JSP swapped into the installed webapp it fails at the display step with the error page; with only the result query put back to entities it fails showing two rows of the first result. | `fixed` |

The routing status line and the toolbar still render, so a clinician can acknowledge a report they cannot read. The button renders whenever the page is opened with `searchProviderNo`, as the patient lab list opens it; the bottom button is intact | Live: the CML lab page opened from the patient lab list listed its buttons as `Acknowledge`, `Forward`, `Close`, `Print`, `Msg`, `Tickler`, `???oscarMDS.segmen` followed on the next line by `tDisplay.btnEChart???`, `Req# ` and then, in the bottom toolbar, a correct `E-Chart`; the PathNet page (BC tables created temporarily) showed the same.
