| 259 | **A lab result observed between 12:00 and 12:59 is filed in `measurements` twelve hours early, so the chart can show an older same-day result as the latest.** `Hl7textResultsData.populateMeasurementsTable` parses the 24-hour observation time (`yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss`, the lab's OBR time) with `new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd hh:mm:ss")` (`:100`, used at `:248`), a 12-hour pattern in which `12` is read as hour 0 of the morning: 12:15 becomes 00:15, while every other hour survives (13:00 is read leniently as 13:00). The same slip builds `dateEntered` from `Calendar.HOUR`, the 0 to 11 hour (`:105`, used at `:239`), so a lab filed at 15:30 is stamped 03:30. The date is right in both when the observation time has seconds (a 14-digit HL7 time, which is all Create Lab writes); a time without seconds (8 or 12 digits) is not parsed at all and is filed as the time of the upload (finding 262). The time of day, which orders same-day results, is not right: the observation time for the noon hour, and the entry time for every afternoon hour | Live: a probe filed seven CML labs through Inbox, Create Lab (2026-10-09), each with one result whose OBR time was 00:30, 11:59, 12:15, 12:59, 13:00, 15:45 or 23:59; `measurements.dateObserved` holds 00:30, 11:59, `00:15`, `00:59`, 13:00, 15:45 and 23:59, while the `datetime` measurementsExt row keeps the true 12:15 and 12:59. Two A1C labs dated 2026-09-30, 6.1 at 08:00 and 7.9 at 12:30, left the chart's A1C line in the Measurements module reading `A1C 6.1 2026-09-30`, the older result. `lab-to-flowsheet-observed-time` files an A1C at 12:15 and reads 00:15. The `dateEntered` half is Source only: the same arithmetic run in the install's JDK gives `03:30:05` for 15:30:05, and the install's clock was before noon, so it was not seen live. Filed as #4616. **Fixed** by #4631 (on release since 14 Oct): the pattern is `HH`, and `dateEntered` uses `Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY`. Live on release/2026.08 at a7611d07b4 (18 Oct), `lab-to-flowsheet-observed-time` passes end to end on an Ontario install: the A1C observed at 12:15 is filed at 12:15, and the Measurements module, the Diabetes Flowsheet and the Health Tracker show the results as before. | `fixed` |
| 260 | **Every consultation service or consultant write regenerates a script that nothing reads, and the consultant, institution and department editors also try to write a file that cannot be written.** `EctConAddService2Action:69-70`, `EctConDeleteServices2Action:75-76`, `EctConDisplayService2Action:80-81` (Update Service Specialists) and the consultant, institution and department editors (`EctConAddSpecialist2Action:119-120`, `EctConEditSpecialists2Action:91-92`, `EctConEditInstitutions2Action:86-87`, `EctConEditDepartments2Action:76-77`) call `EctConConstructSpecialistsScriptsFile.makeString`, which loads every active service (`findActive()`, `:125`) and each one's consultants and rewrites the one `specialistsJavascript` row (`:157-159`). The three editors' display paths do the same each time an entry is opened for editing (`EctConEditSpecialists2Action:136-138`, `EctConEditInstitutions2Action:123-125`, `EctConEditDepartments2Action:93-95`) and first call `makeFile()` (`:65-112`), which opens `encounter/consult.js` relative to the working directory. `ConsultationLookup2Action` replaced the script with AJAX lookups (its Javadoc says so), `EctConConfigurationJavascriptData.getJavascript()` has no caller and no page reads the table or the file, so every one of these calls does its work for no reader | Source: the call sites above, and no reader of `getJavascript()` or `specialistsJavascript` outside `EctConConfigurationJavascriptData`, the DAO and the model. Live: on the packaged install the service's working directory is `/var/lib/carlos-emr/catalina` (under `ProtectSystem=strict`, `ReadWritePaths=/var/lib/carlos-emr`), which has no `encounter/` directory, so `new FileWriter` cannot create the file: it throws an `IOException` that `makeFile()` catches and logs at DEBUG (`:108-109`), a silent no-op. An earlier version of this row also claimed that a deleted service stays named in the script; that was `consultation-services-admin` deleting its own fixture rows by SQL (the application only inactivates a service, and `makeString` lists the active ones), so it was test residue and is withdrawn. Not pinned, on purpose: nothing reads the row or the file; a pin could only assert on an internal table. Low severity. Filed as #4617. **Fixed on `release/2026.08` (#4617):** `EctConConstructSpecialistsScriptsFile` and `EctConConfigurationJavascriptData` are removed with their only remaining dependants, the `SpecialistsJavascript` entity and DAO, and the seven actions no longer call them, so the editors' open paths only read and nothing tries to write `encounter/consult.js` (the `verd` request attribute that carried `makeFile()`'s result, read by no page, is gone too). The `specialistsJavascript` table stays in the schema, where the OSCAR 19 import manifests classify it as reference data; nothing reads or writes it. Pinned after all, now that the pin asserts the fix rather than the dead write: `EctConConfigurationWrites2ActionUnitTest` drives every former call site and fails if one looks up the service list or writes on an editor's open path; `lib/consult-config-state.js` asserts the row's MD5 unchanged instead of restoring it, so `consultation-services-admin` (which now also opens, saves and deletes through the consultant, institution and department editors) and `get-reject-consult-config` fail if any of these writes regenerates it. Live: on a fresh Ubuntu 26.04 install of this branch's packages, `consultation-services-admin`, `consultation-directory-crud`, `specialist-add-cpso` and `get-reject-consult-config` pass; the same `consultation-services-admin` run against the unfixed `release/2026.08` package (e9ce38de) fails at its first write, "Add Service rewrote the legacy specialistsJavascript script". | `fixed` |
| 261 | **The Certain, Uncertain, Major and Not Major radios and the role box of the note editor's unresolved issue checklist are disabled for every login, so a clinician with full rights cannot change an issue's certainty or importance from the chart.** `noteIssueList.jsp:399-417` writes `disabled="${disabled}"` on the four radios and on the role text box of each unresolved issue. EL prints the boolean as the text `false`, and `disabled` is a boolean attribute: its presence disables the control whatever its value, so `disabled="false"` is disabled. The resolved list (`:288-332`) and both checkboxes (`:288`, `:374`) write the attribute conditionally (`${disabled ? 'disabled="disabled"' : ''}`) and are fine; the Acute, Chronic, Resolved and Unresolved radios carry no attribute, so these five controls are the only ones affected. `issueChange` saves certain and major from the posted `issueCheckList[N].issue.*` parameters (finding 229) and no other control offers them, so the only way to set them from the chart is one the page disables. Low to medium severity: the checklist is itself hidden (finding 227), so a clinician first has to find it, and then cannot use half of it | Live: `echart-issues-filter-radios` (2026-10-09, packaged install, the test login `carlosdoc`, full rights): with the checklist revealed by a script (finding 227) and the issue's panel opened, the issue's checkbox and its Acute and Resolved radios are enabled (the control step, which proves the login may write the issue), while the Certain, Uncertain, Major and Not Major radios and the role box all have `disabled === true` and the attribute `disabled="false"`. Source: the lines cited above; `grep -n 'disabled="\${disabled}"' noteIssueList.jsp` finds exactly these five in the unresolved list. Pinned by `echart-issues-filter-radios`. Filed as #4618. | issue-filed |
| 262 | **A lab uploaded with an observation time that has no seconds is filed in `measurements` as observed when it was uploaded, so an old result is listed as today's.** `CMLHandler.formatDateTime` (`lab/ca/all/parsers/CMLHandler.java:595-624`, the message parser, not the upload handler of the same name in `lab/ca/all/upload/handlers/`) turns the OBR-7 observation time into `yyyy-MM-dd` for 8 digits, `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm` for 12 and `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss` for 14. `Hl7textResultsData.populateMeasurementsTable` parses it with `new SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd hh:mm:ss")` (`:100`, used at `:247-250`): a time without seconds does not parse, and the `catch` stores `new Date()`, so `measurements.dateObserved` is the moment of the upload while the `datetime` measurementsExt row keeps the lab's own time. Create Lab always writes 14 digits (`CMLLabHL7Generator` formats `yyyyMMddHHmmss`), which is why no Create Lab check meets it; an uploaded file, the path of a real feed, can carry any of the three widths. The chart's Measurements module titles an A1C `A1C <value> <date of dateObserved>`, and the flowsheet and the Health Tracker list a result under that date, so a result observed in August is shown under today's date and can be taken for the latest. Patient-safety class: a result appears as today's | Live: `lab-to-flowsheet-upload-time` (2026-10-09, packaged install): a CML A1C uploaded through Inbox, HL7 Lab Upload with OBR-7 `202608200945` is stored with `measurementsExt` `datetime` = `2026-08-20 09:45` and `measurements.dateObserved` = the upload time (`2026-10-09 09:03:34`). A probe (not a step of the check) uploaded the same A1C with OBR-7 of 8, 12 and 14 digits (`20260820`, `202608200945`, `20260820094500`): `dateObserved` held the upload time for the first two and `2026-08-20 09:45:00` for the 14-digit one; after the 12-digit upload the chart's Measurements module titled the A1C `A1C 6.1 2026-10-09`. Source: the lines cited above. Pinned by `lab-to-flowsheet-upload-time`. Filed as #4619. **Fixed (#4619):** `Hl7textResultsData.populateMeasurementsTable` parses the observation time with `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm:ss`, then `yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm`, then `yyyy-MM-dd`, so a time without seconds is filed as observed at that time. | fixed |
| 263 | **Enable Request/Response, Update Service Specialists and Update Institution Department write on GET and on HEAD.** `EctConEnableReqResp2Action` (`encounter/EnableConRequestResponse`: privilege check `:62`, writes `:82-90`), `EctConDisplayService2Action` (`encounter/UpdateServiceSpecialists`: `:62`, `:72-77`) and `EctConDisplayInstitution2Action` (`encounter/UpdateInstitutionDepartment`: `:59`, `:67-72`) check no request method, and `HttpMethodGuardFilter` does not refuse them by name: `MUTATOR_ACTION_PREFIXES` (`:83-89`) holds `add` and `del` but neither `enable` nor `update` ("Update" is left out on purpose, `:75-77`), and the match is a `startsWith` on the action name (`:441-444`). None of the three is in `MutatorActionGetRejectionContractUnitTest` either: `io.github.carlos_emr.carlos.encounter.` is not among its `IN_SCOPE_PACKAGE_PREFIXES` (`:510-521`). `AddService` and `DelService` check no method either; the filter refuses them by name. By source, a GET to Enable with no parameters switches both flags off and inactivates Referring Doctor | Live: `get-reject-consult-config` (2026-10-09, packaged install, through the front door) captures the POST each page sends and replays it as GET and as HEAD against rows it owns. All three answer HTTP 200 and write: Enable stores both `property` rows (`consultRequestEnabled`, `consultResponseEnabled`; the rows holding `Y` went from 0 to 2) and creates an active `Referring Doctor` service, Update Service Specialists links the consultant to the service (0 to 1) and Update Institution Department links the department to the institution (0 to 1). `AddService` and `DelService` answer 405 carrying the application's header to both methods and write nothing. Pinned by `get-reject-consult-config-enable-get`, `-update-service-get` and `-update-institution-get`. Split out of 107. Filed as #4620. | `issue-filed` |
| 264 | **The consultation configuration writers authorize on `_con`, a clinical right, instead of the `_admin` / `_admin.consult` that their pages require, and the pages refuse only after the write.** The action gates are `_con` w (`EctConAddService2Action:59`), `_con` u (`EctConDeleteServices2Action:60`, `EctConEnableReqResp2Action:62`) and `_con` r (`EctConDisplayService2Action:62`, `EctConDisplayInstitution2Action:59`). The pages and their result JSPs gate on `<security:oscarSec objectName="_admin,_admin.consult" ... reverse>` and `sendRedirect` to `/securityError` (`AddService.jsp:42-45`, `DeleteServices.jsp:37-40`, `EnableRequestResponse.jsp:37-40`, `ShowAllServices.jsp:55-58`, `ShowAllInstitutions.jsp:39-42`, and the pages that host the two link forms, `DisplayService.jsp:37-40` and `DisplayInstitution.jsp:37-40`), and a result JSP is reached only after the action has written. Both consequences are real: a login holding only `_con` r links consultants to services and departments to institutions, and the seeded `doctor` role (`_con` x, which `SecurityInfoManagerImpl:157-159` answers true for every right) adds and inactivates services and flips the clinic-wide request/response switch by a direct POST, though the pages redirect it to /securityError | Live: `get-reject-consult-config` (2026-10-09, packaged install, through the front door). A throwaway login holding only `_con` r, with its own valid CSRF token, after a control shows the full login's replay of the same request writes: `AddService`, `DelService` and `EnableConRequestResponse` answer 403 with the securityError page and write nothing; `UpdateServiceSpecialists` and `UpdateInstitutionDepartment` write (the link rows went from 0 to 1) and then answer 302 to `/securityError?type=_admin&type=_admin.consult`. A login with the seeded `doctor` role (which holds `_con` x, neither `_admin` nor `_admin.consult`) is first shown to be redirected from the page that hosts each form, and then each of the five replays writes (a service created, a service inactivated, both flags stored with Referring Doctor activated, a consultant link, a department link) and answers the same 302. Pinned by `get-reject-consult-config-update-service-restricted`, `-update-institution-restricted` and the five `-doctor` entries. Split out of 108. Authorization defect for a signed-in user: tracked in umbrella [#4223](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/4223), section A. | `issue-filed` |
| 265 | **The facility chooser's own stylesheet and script are blocked by the pending-facility gate, so every multi-facility sign-in shows the chooser unstyled with two console errors.** `select_facility.jsp` includes `layouts/caisi_html_top.jspf`, whose head links `/css/caisi_css.css` and `/js/global.js` (`:4-5`). While the choice is pending `LoginFilter` turns every route that is not in `PENDING_FACILITY_SELECTION_URLS` (`:222-243`) into a redirect to `/select_facility` (`:495-500`), and the list names the login page's own `/css/Roboto.css`, `/css/fontawesome-all.min.css`, bootstrap, jQuery, flatpickr and `/share/css/` but neither of these two files, so each request is answered 302 to the chooser itself; the browser follows it, receives the chooser's HTML and refuses it as a stylesheet and as a script. The files exist (`src/main/webapp/css/caisi_css.css`, `js/global.js`) and are served once a facility is chosen. `LoginFilterUnitTest` (`shouldPassSupportRoutes_whenFacilitySelectionPending`) pins only bootstrap, `/share/css/`, `/csrfguard` and the heartbeat, so nothing covers the chooser's own head. Low severity: the chooser's forms and buttons need neither file, so a pick still works; the page is unstyled and logs two errors per sign-in, and the strict-page check of every login that passes through the chooser (the harness `login()` clicks through it) records them | Live: `login-facility-chooser` (2026-10-09, packaged install, through the front door), last step "the chooser page's own stylesheet and script are served to a login that has not chosen a facility". A throwaway login in two facilities was sent to the chooser; the stylesheet and script links were read from the chooser page's own DOM and requested with the pending session: `/carlos/css/caisi_css.css -> HTTP 302 to /carlos/select_facility` and `/carlos/js/global.js -> HTTP 302 to /carlos/select_facility`. The control before it (after the pick, same session) answers both 200. The browser reported, on the chooser of each sign-in, "Refused to apply style from '.../select_facility' because its MIME type ('text/html') is not a supported stylesheet MIME type" and "Refused to execute script from '.../select_facility' because its MIME type ('text/html') is not executable", an aborted stylesheet request and a script response served as text/html. Every other step of the check passes (chooser lists exactly the login's facilities; typed Schedule and chart URLs are redirected back; the pick audits one `facilityId=<id>` row; the banner shows only the chosen facility's message; a one-facility login skips the chooser). Pinned by `login-facility-chooser`. Filed as #4621. | `issue-filed` |
| 266 | **Finding a patient and opening the Master Record rewrites the patient's row: a NULL `pronoun` or `gender` becomes an empty string, with no audit row and no `lastUpdateDate`.** `Demographic.getGender()` (`Demographic.java:1226-1231`) and `getPronoun()` (`:1238-1243`) answer `""` when the field is null, and the entity is mapped on its getters (`@jakarta.persistence.Column` sits on the getter, `:1224` and `:1236`), so a patient loaded with a NULL column differs from its loaded state when the request ends and Hibernate flushes an `UPDATE` of the column to `""`. Any read that loads the patient does it: the schedule's quick search and the Master Record page were both seen to. Nothing logs it (the `log` table has no row for it) and `lastUpdateDate` does not move, so the audit trail says the record was only viewed while the row was written. The value means the same to the application, so the harm is small, but a read that writes is a hazard under a replica, a backup comparison and the record-change review the audit exists for, and by 2026-10-09 it had altered the pronoun of 124 of the 3000 demo patients (and the gender of 2) on a validation install, none of them edited. Low severity | Source: the getters and the mapping above. Live: `demographic-open-writes-nothing` (2026-10-09, packaged install, through the front door), last step "the stored row is unchanged after the patient was found and its Master Record opened". An owned patient is stored with NULL pronoun and gender (control step); the schedule Search finds it and its Master Record renders (control step); the same row then reads `pronoun` and `gender` no longer NULL and `lastUpdateDate` unchanged. Also by hand on two demo patients that still had a NULL pronoun (100 and 101): opening the first one's Master Record and typing the second one's surname into the schedule's quick search each turned that patient's `pronoun` from NULL to empty (`lastUpdateDate` 2025-08-11 09:16:00 before and after). A row-by-row comparison of the demo dataset's artifact with the installed database found 109 patients (124 after further runs) whose pronoun, and 2 whose gender, differs only in that way, with `lastUpdateDate` unchanged and none of them among the 25 patients the `log` shows updated. Pinned by `demographic-open-writes-nothing`. Filed as #4622. | `issue-filed` |
| 267 | **A plain doctor is offered Administration ▸ Manage Flowsheets and is refused it.** The Administration panel now opens for the seeded doctor (#4316 admits any `_admin*` read), but `leftNav.jspf:669-671` lists Manage Flowsheets for `_admin,_admin.flowsheet` read and `ManageFlowsheets2Action` (`:60-63`) requires `_admin`, `_admin.misc` or `_admin.flowsheet` write. The doctor role holds `_admin.flowsheet` read only, so the link answers 403. It is the mismatch of #4238 one step further | Live: `admin-role-management` (2026-10-09, 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package, release/2026.08 at 0c43d2f862): after Schedule ▸ Administration opens for the throwaway doctor, `GET admin/ManageFlowsheets` answers 403, so the step "before the role is assigned the throwaway doctor has no Add A Role and is refused it" fails. With that one request replaced by a probe, the check passes through its last step. Source: the nav and action lines above and `database/mysql/migration/on/V1.0.2__on_data.sql:36038`. Filed as #4623. **Fixed (2026-10-19, #4623):** the product decision was to open the page read-only rather than hide the link. The seed's own comment (`updates/update-2026-01-02-add-flowsheet-admin-privilege.sql`) says `_admin.flowsheet` exists so a doctor can reach Manage Flowsheets without `_admin.misc`, `manageFlowsheets.jsp` already admitted read, and the doctor holds `_flowsheet` `x`, which is what the Edit link needs. `ManageFlowsheets2Action` now asks for read to render the list and for write to change a flowsheet (a POST carrying `method`); it publishes `flowsheetWriteAccess`, the page shows the Enable/Disable links and the custom flowsheet upload only when that is true, and the page no longer repeats the access check in a combined `oscarSec` tag over `_admin`, `_admin.misc` and `_admin.flowsheet` (that tag keeps one privilege per role, so a `_admin.misc` `o` row could mask `_admin.flowsheet` `r` and bounce a reader the action had admitted; reproduced on the package and now pinned). The seeds are unchanged, so a doctor cannot enable, disable or upload until an administrator grants `_admin.flowsheet` write. Pinned by `ManageFlowsheets2ActionUnitTest` and by `manage-flowsheets-access`, which fails against the unfixed action and page. Not fixed here: on the 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package built from release/2026.08 at fefceecc62 the seeded doctor's day-sheet Administration link is not rendered at all, because its 13-object tag has the same one-privilege-per-role limit and the doctor's `_admin.reporting` `o` row (V1.0.6) masks `_admin.flowsheet` `r` in the order that install's database returns the rows; so `admin-role-management`'s step for the doctor's Administration panel is skipped by its own `#admin-panel` guard on such an install, and `manage-flowsheets-access` opens the shell by its address instead. **Day-sheet link fixed by #4328:** `appointmentprovideradminday.jsp` and `mainMenu.jsp` include `administration-menu-gate.jspf`, which checks the same thirteen objects with one tag each, so that row can no longer hide the link; `admin-role-management` now requires the link for the seeded doctor instead of skipping its panel step without it. The same PR gates System Management, Manage Flowsheets (read) and Assign Role/Rights to Object (write, menu and home-page card) one object at a time. `admin-role-management` no longer carries `expectedFailure`, and `authz-read-role-matrix` no longer lists `admin/ManageFlowsheets` as a refusal for the seeded doctor. | `fixed` |
| 268 | **Job Type Management raises a DataTables "Cannot reinitialise DataTable" alert after every save.** `jobTypes.jsp` removes the table rows and calls `listJobs()`, whose `getJobTypes()` runs `initiate()` and so calls `.DataTable(...)` on `#jobTypeTable` a second time | Live: `admin-jobs` (2026-10-09, packaged install): every earlier step passes, then the check fails on an unexpected alert, "DataTables warning: table id=jobTypeTable - Cannot reinitialise DataTable". Source: `jobTypes.jsp:113-160`. Tracked in #3402. | `issue-filed` |

## 15. Alpha19 tester report: Preventions Enable Print, and adding vaccines (October 2026)

Reported against `2026.08.0-alpha19` with no further detail: "Preventions section doesn't allow
enable print to work and still has when adding vaccines". Reproduced on `release/2026.08` against
Tomcat with the Ontario demo data, and through the shipped nginx/ModSecurity/CRS policy with
`scripts/waf/check-clinical-policy.py`'s engine. Pinned by `prevention-print`. Findings from 273
on were raised as follow-ups on the same pull request.

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 269 | **On the packaged install, Print is refused with 403 for any patient with an ordinary clinical comment on a prevention.** The Print form repeated every record's comments, reasons, vaccine names and lots as hidden fields (`preventProcedure*<i>-<k>`), so `/prevention/printPrevention` carried the chart's free text, and only `/prevention/AddPrevention` has the prose exclusion (id 1117). A comment that saved fine ("Reviewed labs with the patient; find attached the CBC and lytes.") made the whole print fail | Live, real CRS engine: 5 of the 7 `clinical-prose-corpus` phrases in `preventProcedureComments34-0` were refused on the print route (rules 932100/932110, 932130, 930100/930110, 931100, 933100) while the same text was allowed on AddPrevention. **Fixed:** the form posts only the ticked type names and `PreventionPrintLoader` reads the printout from the chart; a selection naming every configured type passes the same engine | fixed |
| 270 | **Enter in the Preventions page's vaccine box prints.** The "Pick vaccine brand/generic" (and CVC lot) box sat inside the Print form, so Enter with no suggestion highlighted submitted it: a `Prevention.pdf` download, a "check at least one prevention" alert, or on the packaged install the 403 page in place of the Preventions page | Live: typing `adac` and Enter posted `/prevention/printPrevention` and downloaded `Prevention.pdf`. **Fixed:** the form refuses implicit submission; Enter opens the highlighted suggestion, else the first one shown, else nothing | fixed |
| 271 | **Print Immunizations Only with nothing ticked, or any failed print, saved the page's HTML as `Prevention.pdf`.** `printImmOnly()` skipped the selection check, and `PreventionPrint2Action` set the PDF `Content-Disposition` before generating, then answered its `error` result (the Preventions page) under it; the page's print-error banner also showed only when there were recommendations | Live: the download's first bytes were `<!DOCTYPE html`. **Fixed:** both buttons check the selection; the PDF is drawn into a buffer and checked for `%PDF-` before any header; failures answer the page with the banner (400 nothing printable, 500 drawing failed) | fixed |
| 272 | **The printout could show the wrong prevention, "HIN: (ON) null null", and never its MRP line.** Boxes in "show/hide all other Preventions" were numbered by that list's position while the hidden fields used the configuration position; the header printed null health card parts; the MRP line came from the session `providerBean` (filled only by the day schedule; a NullPointerException without it) and overflowed the fixed 60pt header box | Source and `PreventionPrintPdfUnitTest`. **Fixed:** boxes carry the type name; blank HIN parts are left out; the MRP is the patient's own, by `mrp_model`, and the top margin grows by a line for it | fixed |
| 273 | **`prevention_show_comments` did not hide comments on the printout, and printed them twice when on.** The PDF printed each record's comment under a "Comments:" label whatever the setting said, then again in its own block when the setting was on; the Preventions page itself honours it (inline when on, a footnote marker when off) | Live: with the shipped default (`false`) the page footnoted the Inf comment while the PDF printed it; a unit print with the setting on had the comment twice in the extracted text. **Fixed:** `PreventionPrintLoader` reads comments only when the setting is on and the PDF prints them once; `prevention-print` asserts the PDF matches the page in both settings (run with each) | fixed |
| 274 | **On the packaged install, saving a prevention is refused with 403 when the "never" reason, the "specify location" box or an outside provider's name holds ordinary clinical prose.** Exclusion 1117 covered only `comments`, `reason` and `reasonOtherText` on `/prevention/AddPrevention` | Live, real CRS engine: 5 of the 7 `clinical-prose-corpus` phrases were refused in each of `neverReason`, `location2` and `providerName` (rules 932100/932110, 932130, 930100/930110, 931100, 933100). **Fixed:** 1117 removes the six content-attack families from those three names too (XSS stays on); `check-clinical-policy.py` now posts the corpus through every prevention prose field and keeps the lot, the route, the method and XSS inspected | fixed |
| 275 | **Chinese, Japanese and Korean characters and emoji silently dropped out of the prevention PDF.** The embedded DejaVu faces have no CJK and few emoji glyphs, and OpenPDF leaves out a character its font cannot draw, so a patient's or outside provider's name printed with gaps | Source and unit: `PreventionPrintPdf` drew chart text in DejaVu only; a unit print of "FAKE-陈, 小明", "김민준" and "Tolerated well 👍" extracted as their Latin parts only. **Fixed:** `PdfFonts.addText` draws each character the font lacks in bundled WenQuanYi Micro Hei (CJK, Hangul) or Symbola 2.60 (monochrome emoji to Unicode 9), and marks a character no face has with U+FFFD; the printout routes every chart value through it, and `prevention-print` saves an outside provider named in all three scripts through the add form and finds it in the PDF. Newer emoji (Unicode 10+, e.g. 🧪) print as U+FFFD | fixed |

## 16. Found while extending scheduling coverage (October 2026)

Verified by reading `develop` at `0422b8dda1`. 276 was then confirmed live (15 Oct 2026, private
Tomcat, `multisites=on`) and is pinned by `admin-manage-sites` (expectedFailure), which SKIPs on
installs with `multisites=off`. 277 and 278 stay `needs-live-check`: they are routes with no UI
entry, so per the suite's rule they get no check.

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 276 | **Satellite-sites Admin cannot save, open or edit a site.** (a) The detail form posts `site.name`, `site.shortName`, `site.bgColor` and the other `site.*` fields (`sitesAdminDetail.jsp:94-157`), but `SitesManage2Action` exposes the site only as `s` (`getS`/`setS`, `:110-118`), so nothing binds and `save()` dereferences a null site (`:86-89`). (b) The list opens a site through a GET link with `method=update` (`sitesAdmin.jsp:93`), and `HttpMethodGuardFilter` refuses any GET whose `method` is `update` (`MUTATOR_METHOD_PARAMS`, `:133`). (c) The detail form has no `value` attributes and a blank hidden `site.siteId` (`sitesAdminDetail.jsp:160`), so an opened site would show empty fields and its Save would add a new site. The page exists only with `multisites=on` (`admin.jsp:701`, `administration/leftNav.jspf:617`). | Source reading; `scripts/admin-manage-sites-playwright-checks.js` asserts the working behaviour and is expected to fail at "Add New Site ▸ Save stores the new site and lists it". Live on develop `0422b8dda1` with `multisites=on` (15 Oct 2026): Save renders an error page and stores no site, as (a) predicts; (b) and (c) sit behind it and are from source reading. Filed as #4700. **Fixed** by #4875: the site binds as `site`, the list opens a site with the read-only `method=edit`, and the detail form shows the stored values. With #4922 (the site pages' stylesheet link) also on release, `admin-manage-sites` passes end to end. | `fixed` |
| 277 | **`tickler/AddTickler` has no UI entry.** Every Add Tickler control opens `tickler/ViewAddTickler` (`casemgmt/navigation.jsp:326`, `tickler/ticklerDemoMain.jsp:1102`, `documentManager/showDocument.jsp:512`, `ViewConsultationRequests.jsp:1017`), whose page posts `tickler/DbTicklerAdd`. `AddTickler2Action` (`struts-scheduling.xml:76`) is named only in `ViewAddTickler2Action`'s Javadoc. | Source search of every JSP, JSPF, JS and Java file for the route. | `needs-live-check` |
| 278 | **The clinic waiting-list pages have no UI entry.** `waitinglist/WLEditWaitingListNameAction` (`struts-scheduling.xml:58`) is reached only from `DisplayWaitingList.jsp:255`, which only `waitinglist/SetupDisplayWaitingList` (`:54`) renders, and nothing outside those pages opens that route; the flip view's `originalpage=waitingList` branch (`scheduleflipview.jsp:99-101`) has no caller either. Patients are still added and removed through the Master Record (`waitinglist/SetupDisplayPatientWaitingList`, `Add2WaitingList`, `RemoveFromWaitingList`), which `waiting-list` covers; list names can only be created by SQL. | Source search, as for 277; `waiting-list-playwright-checks.js` records the same gap in its header. | `needs-live-check` |

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 279 | **Editing a signed chart note and pressing Sign & Save answers HTTP 500, and the edit is not stored.** In the reopened chart, the signed note's Edit link opens it for editing, and Sign & Save (`savePage('saveAndExit')`) posts `observation_date` as a raw timestamp (`2026-10-15 19:03:06.0`, the `java.sql.Timestamp.toString()` form). `CaseManagementEntry2Action.noteSave` (`:1671`) parses it with `dd-MMM-yyyy H:mm` and throws `ParseException`, which the exception interceptor answers with 500. A new note posts `15-Oct-2026 19:03` and saves. The input the edit form renders is formatted (`noteIssueList.jsp:113-115`); where the raw value comes from is not traced yet. | Live: `echart-note-lifecycle` fails at "Sign & Save of the edit stores a new revision with the same uuid and keeps the first one unchanged", and its message carries the posted value (2 of 2 runs). `echart-note-soft-wrap` fails the same way at "Edit-link editor soft-wraps and Sign & Save stores only the typed line breaks" (2 of 3 runs here). Source: `noteSave` parse at `CaseManagementEntry2Action.java:1671`. Not yet compared with the devcontainer. Filed as #4711. Fixed on release/2026.08 by #4769 (0a4e3cc825), so `echart-note-lifecycle` carries no expectedFailure pin there. | `fixed` |

Seen live on `develop` at `0422b8dda1` (15 Oct 2026, private Tomcat on JDK 25 over loopback HTTPS,
throwaway Ontario and BC schemas, FAKE data).

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 280 | **Delete on a saved reading's page (Diabetes Flowsheet ▸ reading ▸ Add Measurement Data) is refused with 403, so a saved measurement cannot be deleted.** For a saved reading (`id` present), `AddMeasurementData.jsp:608-610` disables every element of `#measurementForm` and enables only `#deleteButton` and `#deleteCheck` again. CSRFGuard's hidden `CSRF-TOKEN` field is already in the form, so it stays disabled: the patched client only refreshes an existing field's value and never enables it (`carlos-csrfguard.js:643`). A disabled field is left out of the submission, so `DeleteData2` arrives without a token, `CarlosCsrfGuardFilter` refuses it, the user sees the error page and the reading stays. The GET refusal and the read-only page are fine | Live: `flowsheet-measurement-delete` (2026-10-15, develop 0422b8dda1, private Tomcat over HTTPS, Ontario throwaway schema): after the page loads, the form's one `CSRF-TOKEN` field is disabled (its value is set); the Delete POST carries only `deleteCheckbox` and `delete`; CARLOS answers 403 and logs `CSRF violation ... Required Token is missing from the Request`; the reading is still in `measurements` and no `measurementsDeleted` copy is written. Filed as #4715. Fixed on release/2026.08 by #4774 (88fd53cce6), which landed its own `flowsheet-measurement-delete` check; the check pinned to this finding on develop (expectedFailure at its Delete step) is not carried over. | `fixed` |

Nothing sets that attribute any more: `billingBC.jsp:326` removes it on every fresh open, and `BillingBCSetup2Action` builds a `BillingCreateBilling2Form` instead. The unguarded call dates from the WEB-INF move (#1632, 2026-04-14). Not among #4439's mapped defects (D7, the form's missing name, sits behind it) | Live: `billing-bc-bill-create-save` (2026-10-15, develop 0422b8dda1, private Tomcat, BC throwaway schema, billregion=BC): Master Record ▸ Create Invoice opens `billing?billRegion=BC&…&appointment_no=0`, which renders the error page; the server log has `NullPointerException: Cannot invoke "…BillingCreateBillingForm.getXml_billtype()" because "thisForm" is null` from the JSP. Filed as #4716. Fixed on release/2026.08 by #4723 (7db01abb4f), so `billing-bc-bill-create-save` carries no expectedFailure pin there. | `fixed` |

The empty input dates from #1632 | Live: `billing-bc-private-bill-receipt` (2026-10-15, develop 0422b8dda1, BC throwaway schema): Bill Status ▸ invoice ▸ receipt ▸ Update Invoice renders the error page; the server log has `NumberFormatException: For input string: ""` at `BillingmasterDAO.getBillingMasterByBillingNo`. The check's earlier steps (Bill Status, receipt, 405 for a GET) pass. Filed as #4717. Fixed on release/2026.08 by #4723 (7db01abb4f), and finding 164 by #4659 (7464260160), so `billing-bc-private-bill-receipt` carries no expectedFailure pin there. | `fixed` |

The clinic-wide Bill Status (Administration ▸ Billing ▸ Edit Invoices) filters correctly. Either hide or disable the radios in the patient view, or apply them there too | Live: `billing-bc-bill-status-filters` (2026-10-15, develop 0422b8dda1, BC throwaway schema): in the Invoice List, Not Submitted listed the owned not-submitted, settled and bill-patient bills alike; on the clinic-wide page each status listed exactly its own bill. The check now asserts the filters on the clinic-wide page and only that the Invoice List lists the patient's bills. | `open` |

`release/2026.08` was already using those numbers for sections 16 and 17, and renumbered
to 284 to 286 when the release line was merged into `main` (#4913).

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 284 | Ontario billing marked a review token saved before appointment update and corrected-bill cleanup; a partial failure made retries silently skip those writes | Source: #4905 moves the dependent business writes in BillingOnSave2Action into one transaction before marking the token saved. BillingOnSaveTransactionIntegrationTest exercises appointment exceptions/false returns and cleanup exceptions/refusals with real rollback and retry/repeat checks; BillingOnSave2ActionUnitTest covers cleanup false returns. | fixed |
| 285 | Non Rostered Only on the report day sheet still lists a rostered patient | Live report-daysheet-labs on the packaged Ubuntu 26.04 stack: after all PHCP role-security assertions passed, the final Non Rostered Only step listed the owned rostered patient’s self/visit appointments. The pre-existing scripts/playwright-suite.json notes already identify this ignored filter. Outside #4905’s seven selected fixes; retained for release disposition.  **Fixed in #4910:** the complete live report-daysheet-labs workflow passes on Ubuntu 26.04, including provider/group/all-provider exclusions, NR/TE/blank/NULL inclusion, every sort link preserving provider/dates/time/roster filtering, and unknown sort-key fallback. | fixed |
| 286 | Additional stored-markup sinks remain outside the shared demographic name/age tag | Live xss-poison-echart after #4905: raw tickler messages and eForm instance names became markup in chart navigation; patient first/last names became markup in /rx/showAllergy; a stored consultation letterhead name broke inline JavaScript in /encounter/ViewRequest. The original walk reported six findings including the resulting script error. The shared DemographicNameAgeTag sink is separately fixed under finding 117. **Fixed in #4910 on the #4902 release branch:** the complete live Ubuntu 26.04 E-Chart walk passes with 36 destinations opened, zero inert links and zero encoding/script findings. Four existing optional prevention-due entries disappear after module refresh. Long-title links fit their visible navbar area, titles and dates open their destinations, and the harness exits inspected in-place CPP editors before continuing. | fixed |

reproduction and separately records fixes already merged into the release.

## 20. Found while extending workflow, task and waiting-list coverage (18 October 2026)

Target source: `release/2026.08` at `a7611d07b4a`. The checks assert the desired
behaviour; a source finding stays `needs-live-check` until its actual browser
path has been exercised. Package setup and run results are recorded in
[UI path coverage validation](ui-path-coverage-2026-10-18.md).

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 287 | **The WorkFlow queue's RH link escapes the application context.** A queue row should open that patient's saved pregnancy form. `RHWorkFlow.getLink()` returns `../form/forwardshortcutname`, while `WorkFlowList.jsp` sets its base to the application root; the browser resolves the link outside `/carlos`. | Live: the packaged Ubuntu 26.04 build opens `https://127.0.0.1/form/forwardshortcutname`, outside `/carlos`, from the queue row. | 288 | **RH Form's Add Injection button omits the form route prefix.** It opens `addRhInjection` relative to the application-root base; the allowed view is `form/addRhInjection`. The button should open an injection editor carrying the patient, form and workflow. | Live: Add Injection renders the CARLOS error page on the packaged Ubuntu 26.04 build. Source: `formRhImmuneGlobulin.jsp`'s Add Injection onclick and `FormViewRoutes.ALLOWED_WILDCARD_FORM_VIEWS`. `rh-injection-workflow` fails at “Add Injection opens for the saved RH form and the same patient and workflow”. Tracking audit 2026-10-18: [#4992](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/4992); newly filed with related findings grouped in the ticket. **Fixed (2026-10-22, #4992):** Add Injection opens `form/addRhInjection`. The packaged install then showed `HttpMethodGuardFilter` answering the editor's GET with 405 (its name starts with `add`); the view is now listed as read-only. `rh-injection-workflow` passes on debs built from the fix; its pin is removed. | fixed |
| 289 | **The task list's Deleted filter leaves the previous status view's action buttons.** Changing the filter only reloads the DataTable; Complete/Delete versus Erase Completely are chosen by JSP at page render. The Deleted view should offer Erase Completely and hide Complete/Delete. | Live: `tickler-bulk-state-actions` on the packaged Ubuntu 26.04 build completes its selection, Complete, explicit refresh/reactivation and Delete steps; it then fails at “the Deleted filter offers Erase Completely and hides Complete and Delete”. The one deleted task is correctly listed, but Erase Completely is absent. Source: `ticklerMain.jsp`'s ticklerview change listener and tablefoot conditional. Tracking audit 2026-10-18: [#4993](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/4993); newly filed with related findings grouped in the ticket. | issue-filed |
| 290 | **Save Injection's submit handler returns an undefined `False` identifier.** JavaScript uses lowercase `false`; executing `return False` raises ReferenceError after the handler fills the reason. A successful clinical save should complete without a script error. | Source: `addRhInjection.jsp:process`. The strict recorder in `rh-injection-workflow`'s “Save Injection stores one RH prevention and refreshes the form panel without a script error” step detects the error. Live confirmation is blocked while finding 288 prevents the real button from opening the editor; no direct-address workaround is counted as UI coverage. Tracking audit 2026-10-18: [#4992](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/4992); newly filed with related findings grouped in the ticket. **Fixed (2026-10-22, #4992):** the handler returns `true`. Live, Save then answered 400: `prevention/AddPrevention` refused the RH type because the Preventions page does not list it. The action now accepts it, and `rh-injection-workflow`'s Save step passes on debs built from the fix. | fixed |
| 291 | **Existing RH injections disappear from the RH form, so their panel Delete controls cannot be reached.** The workflow-linked prevention is stored, but the injection fragment answers HTTP 200 with only whitespace. `getPreventionDataFromExt` passes the prevention entity's `toString()` to `getPreventionById`, which expects a numeric id; the NumberFormatException is swallowed, followed by a null-map error. | Live: `rh-injection-existing` on the packaged Ubuntu 26.04 build stores one owned RH prevention and its workflow/form extension links, reopens the form through the chart, receives the panel's HTTP 200, and fails at “a saved RH form displays an existing injection with its lot, product, dose and reason”. Journal correlation shows `PreventionData.java:240`, `:470` and `:241`; source confirms the entity-versus-id mistake. Tracking audit 2026-10-18: [#4992](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/4992); newly filed with related findings grouped in the ticket. **Fixed (2026-10-22, #4992):** `getPreventionDataFromExt` passes the prevention id. The panel, shown again, encodes its values and lists only the chart's patient, and its Delete posts the prevention type and date `prevention/AddPrevention` requires (it answered 400 without them). `rh-injection-existing` passes on debs built from the fix; its pin is removed. | fixed |

Both preserve an untouched saved control, leave the draft absent after reload, create no prescription and clean all owned rows. Source: `RxWriteScript2Action.updateDrugLocked` uses the null result of `getStashItem2` in `updateQty` and `parseInstructions`; the latter logs null and dereferences it anyway. The independent log audit stays unsuppressed. Latest release `fefceecc62aa` has the same affected source. Grouped tracking: [#5036](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/5036), after open/closed issue and PR searches found no matching tracker. | issue-filed |

## 23. Found while resolving #4568: the CML report-version chain (October 2026)

Row 313 was reproduced on `carlos-emr 2026.09.0~snapshot26` built from the #4568 fix branch and installed
into an Ubuntu 26.04 container, by the matching step of `cml-version-chain`. Row 314 was found by an
independent review of the fix and is read from source.

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 313 | **Opening the patient-search popup from a legacy CML report logs two ERROR entries with stack traces, and its Add as New Patient form is never pre-filled from the report.** `PatientSearch.jsp:107-127` hands `labNo` to `Factory.getHandler(String)` (`Factory.java:76-89`), the factory for HL7 text messages, whatever `labType` the popup was opened for. A legacy CML report is not an `hl7TextMessage`, so the `find` returns null: the factory logs "Could not retrieve lab for segmentID" with a `NullPointerException` (`:85`), falls back to a handler for an empty message, which logs an `EncodingNotSupportedException` (`:224`) and returns null, and the JSP's own `catch` (`:126`) swallows the next NPE and leaves the form blank. The MDS and PathNet pages open the same popup (`SegmentDisplay.jsp:219` passes `labType=MDS`, the BC `labDisplay.jsp:171` passes `BCP`); only the HL7 pages (`lab/CA/ALL/labDisplay.jsp` and `labDisplayAjax.jsp`) pass `HL7`, so the same call fails for them (source; not driven live). Where an HL7 lab carries the same number as the report, the form would instead be pre-filled from that other lab's patient (name, birth date, sex, address, phone, health number), because the tables number their rows independently (source; not driven live). The page already receives `labType`; asking the factory only for `HL7` would end both | Live: the matching step of `scripts/cml-version-chain-playwright-checks.js` (2026-10-15, packaged install, through the front door) opens the popup from an owned legacy CML report and searches. The journal logged, for the open and again for the search, `ERROR parsers.Factory (Factory.java:85) - Could not retrieve lab for segmentID(n)` (`NullPointerException: Cannot invoke "Hl7TextMessage.getType()" because "hl7TextMessage" is null`, thrown in `Factory.getHandler` and called from `PatientSearch_jsp._jspService`), then `WARN ... No message handler specified for type:` and `ERROR ... Could not create message handler` (`:224`, `EncodingNotSupportedException`). The page rendered and the match succeeded. The server-log audit printed the first as an unexplained signature and the second under the malformed-upload entry. Follow-up in #4670 restricts the factory to HL7 in both the popup and the patient-list service-date loader; the broad missing-HL7 baseline entry was removed. Legacy Add as New Patient continues to open without HL7 prefill. | `fixed` |
| 314 | **At initial review, a report's version chain was keyed on its accession number alone, for every legacy lab type: reports of two patients that share one within four months are acknowledged, filed, forwarded and patient-matched as one order.** The CML (`LabPatientPhysicianInfoDaoImpl.findLabServiceDatesByLabId`), MDS (`MdsMSHDaoImpl.findLabsByAccessionNumAndId`), PathNet (`PathnetResultsData.getMatchingLabs` through `Hl7PidDao.findByFillerOrderNumber`) and HL7 (`Hl7TextInfoDaoImpl.findByLabIdViaMagic`) lookups select on the accession number, and the resolvers then keep what lies within four months of the selected report; none compares the patient. Acknowledge files the older versions for the provider, Forward routes every version, and the strict patient match (`CommonLabResultData.updatePatientLabRouting`, `:708-727`) removes each version's `patientLabRouting` row and links it to the patient chosen for the one report that was opened, so a report that belongs to someone else moves to the wrong chart. Nothing bounds the chain either: a placeholder accession that is not blank (a `0`) joins every report that carries it, and `CMLDisplay.jsp` copies the whole chain into each `Version:` link. The design rests on a vendor not reusing an accession number within four months, which is what the window is for, and a corrected report may legitimately carry corrected patient details, so a same-patient condition needs thought rather than a one-line guard. For CML this was dormant while finding 197 hid the chain; the #4568 fix lets CML behave as the other lab types always have | Source: the four lookups, their resolvers and `CommonLabResultData:708-727`. An independent review of #4670 confirmed it by running the production DAO and HQL against H2: two CML reports with different patient names, one accession and 20 days between their service dates came back as one two-report chain from both the best-effort and the strict form, and 3000 reports sharing the accession `0` as a 3000-report chain. Not driven on a packaged install, and the HL7, MDS and PathNet lookups were read, not run. CML guarded in #4670; other sources remain open. | `open` |

## 24. Found while resolving #3394: writes named by a parameter other than `method` (October 2026)

Reproduced on `carlos-emr 2026.09.0~snapshot26` built from the #3394 fix branch and installed into an
Ubuntu 26.04 container (packaged nginx/ModSecurity front door), by `get-reject-dispatch-params` with the
pre-fix classes swapped into the installed webapp, then passing on the fixed classes.

| # | Defect | Evidence | Status |
|---|---|---|---|
| 315 | **Four routes wrote on a GET because the write rides on a parameter other than `method`.** `HttpMethodGuardFilter` refuses a GET only by the route name's verb or the `method` value, and none of these actions checked the method for the parameter that names its write: `admin/ProviderPrivilege` `buttonUpdate` (merges a role's privilege bits; the gate checked only `submit`, the #2862 exploit), `admin/ProviderRole` `buttonUpdate` and `buttonSetPrimaryRole` (and `submit` in any non-English UI: the gate compared it with English `add`/`delete` while the JSP compares the localized label), `messenger/HandleMessages` `delete`, and `report/DxresearchReport` `method=editDesc` (rewrites a shared coding-system description; `editdesc` starts with no verb the filter knows). Provider Role writes also needed only read privilege, and `editDesc` only `_report` read | Live, pre-fix classes: the check performs each write by the real page's POST, then replays it as GET and HEAD against a second owned row. 6 of 9 replays answered 200; the GET wrote privilege row B, the throwaway provider's role, deleted message B and rewrote the owned ICD-9 description. Fixed classes: all 9 answer 405 with the application's header and change nothing; every POST still writes. Fixed by #3394: each action refuses the GET itself, the filter lists the write parameters per route as a backstop, and `MutatorActionGetRejectionContractUnitTest` drives every listed route and parameter against its action. | `fixed` |

Edit Appointment ▸ Print Card (`appointment/appointmentviewrecordcard.jsp:201-213`) reads `APPT_CARD_NAME`, `APPT_CARD_PHONE` and `APPT_CARD_FAX`, which nothing writes, so every card shows "Dr." and the provider's own name with the clinic's phone and fax, whatever the provider saved. | Live: `appointment-card-preferences` (21 Oct 2026, release build 1aee7c707a, ON) passes its Save All and Print Card steps and fails at the last: the card reads "Dr. Throwaway …" with the clinic's phone and fax, none of the three saved values. link from being a working workflow.

|---|---|---|---|
| 317 | **Ontario hospital (shortcut) billing had no opener.** #654 removed the Master Record sidebar's billing links, saying they were all still in the main navigation; Hospital Billing was not. Issue [#5055](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/5055). | 318 | **The hospital-billing service-date calendar threw on every click, so no hospital bill could be dated.** Its field is a read-only textarea named, not identified, `billDate`, so `Calendar.setup` found no input field and bound Flatpickr to the trigger link. Flatpickr opened on the click the shim's trigger handler then toggled shut, its key and blur handlers read a `.value` the link lacks (`trimEnd` TypeError), and the page's `onClose` ends with `cal.hide()`, which re-entered Flatpickr's unguarded `close()` until "Maximum call stack size exceeded". | Live: 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package with only row 317 fixed, Ubuntu 26.04 container: `billing-on-hospital-shortcut` failed at "the service-date calendar records the picked date" with both page errors and an empty date box. Source: `share/calendar/calendar-setup.js` (`target = inputEl \|\| buttonEl`; `hide: instance.close()`). Fixed for #5055: with no field the shim binds Flatpickr to a hidden stand-in field placed at the trigger, and `cal.hide()` closes only an open calendar. `calendar-eform-browser-check.js` runs the page's own service-date script on the shipped Flatpickr and fails on the previous shim; `calendar-setup-keyboard.test.js` models the unguarded `close()`. | `fixed` |
| 319 | **Save on the hospital-billing Confirmation reopened page 1 instead of closing the window,** so a saved bill looked unsaved and invited a second one. The Confirmation's buttons are named `button`; `BillingShortcutPg2Service` decided from `submit`, which the page echoes from page 1 and which always reads "Next". | Live: same package with rows 317 and 318 fixed: `billing-on-hospital-shortcut` saved the claim, then failed at "Save writes one claim for this patient and closes the window" (no close within 20 s; page 1 reloaded). Source: `BillingShortcutPg2Service.assemble` and `billingShortcutPg2.jsp`'s buttons. Fixed for #5055: the decision reads `button` (falling back to `submit`); `BillingShortcutPg2ServicePostSaveActionUnitTest`. | `fixed` |
| 320 | **In any locale but English, hospital billing never priced or saved a bill.** Page 1's Next was a submit `<input>`, which posts its label (`billing.hospitalBilling.btnNext`: "Suivant", "Siguiente", "Dalej", "Próximo"). `BillingShortcutPg2Service` calculates and saves only when `submit` is Next, Save or Save and Back, and the Confirmation echoes page 1's value back, so the Confirmation came up without a price and Save wrote nothing. | Live: 2026.09.0~snapshot26 package of this fix with the release `billingShortcutPg1.jsp` compiled: the `billing-on-hospital-shortcut` step with a fr-CA browser failed at "The French Next did not price the Confirmation". Source: `billingShortcutPg1.jsp`'s submit control and `BillingShortcutPg2Service.shouldSave`; reported by the Codex review of #5172. Fixed for #5055: Next is a `<button value="Next">` whose text stays translated; `BillingShortcutPg1NextButtonJspUnitTest` fails on the release page, and the fr-CA step passes. | `fixed` |

## How this list is meant to be used

1. A finding here is **not** a reason to weaken a check. The suite's rule is
   report, don't encode: a check that pins current broken behaviour as expected
   makes the bug permanent.
2. When an issue is filed, set the Status cell to `issue-filed` with the issue
   number, and update the console-baseline entry's `issue` field to the same
   number so the burn-down is traceable from either direction. Filing does not
   upgrade a `needs-live-check` finding: those stay as they are until a live pass
   confirms them.
3. When a fix lands, delete the console-baseline entry in the same change —
   otherwise the suite stays blind to the next occurrence.
4. **This file is enforced, not remembered.** `scripts/app-findings-log.test.js`
   runs in CI and fails the build if a finding has no evidence or an unknown
   status, if ids are not unique and consecutive, or — the point of it — if a
   console-baseline entry cites a finding that is not recorded here. The suite
   may not tolerate a defect that nobody wrote down.

## Adding a finding

Keep the same shape as the rows above: the next id, what the defect is, the
evidence (a command, a file and line, or the check that caught it — enough for a
reader to re-check it), and a status. If a browser check had to tolerate the
defect to keep running, add the console-baseline entry in the same change and
point its `issue` field at this finding by number.

If the finding is an authorization defect for a signed-in user (a role or a patient
restriction that is not honoured, or a refusal that is not clean), add it to the
umbrella [#4223](https://github.com/carlos-emr/carlos/issues/4223) and say so in the evidence
cell, as the rows above do.

### CML follow-up hardening (19 October 2026)

The #4670 follow-up merges `release/2026.08` at `da1442049775`, preserving its findings 276–312 and renumbering this PR's findings to 313–314.

CML candidates now include source identity and nullable print headers. A matching nonblank HIN needs corroborating DOB or full name; when a HIN is absent, DOB and full name must match. Conflicting HINs, insufficient identity, unreadable service dates and missing or malformed in-window headers make the chain unavailable. The report still opens with a visible warning and only its own ID. Acknowledge, File, Forward and patient matching refuse ambiguous chains before writing. Current chart assignments are not identity evidence, so valid versions routed to different charts remain together for correction. Source corrections that remove all matching identity evidence require review; they are not silently omitted.

The service-date window uses `LocalDate`, retaining the legacy repeated-month-end clamping and strict four-month cutoff without midnight/DST conversion. Forward preflights all selected chains and commits routing together; CML File commits each complete chain together. Failure-injection tests prove rollback of earlier version writes. Acknowledgement failures return a non-success HTTP status.

The chart and patient lab list now collapse CML versions with source-qualified IDs and keep the newest visible version when a later one is outside the patient list. The browser check now also forwards both versions through the provider picker and verifies an explicit warning for conflicting identities. The CML patient search and lab-list date lookup never pass legacy report numbers to the HL7 factory. The validation run and its deployment scope are recorded in `deb-install-validation.md`.
